Building on SciDocIR, we construct SciDocDataset, comprising approximately 15K supervised fine-tuning samples and 8K reinforcement-learning samples across 14 verifiable subtasks. Together, SciDocBench, SciDocIR, and SciDocDataset form an evaluation-to-training framework for diagnosing and improving scientific-document assistants. The project page is available at https://github.com/InternLM/SciDocBench.

</details>


### [305] [A Hybrid Predictive Ensemble of Machine Learning and Deep Neural Networks for Early Cardiovascular Disease Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05146)
*Balaji Venkateswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个智能框架，集成机器学习与深度神经网络集成技术，利用IoMT设备实时采集生理数据，通过预处理、特征选择和SVM、随机森林、XGBoost等分类器的集成架构，实现心血管疾病的早期检测与预后，并在云基础设施上支持实时监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病的早期检测和预后对于改善患者预后至关重要，传统方法在诊断精度、误报率和一致性方面存在不足，需要更智能、可扩展的检测框架。

Method: 整合IoMT设备实时生理数据，采用噪声去除、归一化和缺失值填补等预处理步骤，通过有效特征选择识别关键健康指标，再使用SVM、随机森林和XGBoost等分类器构建集成架构，基于云基础设施实现实时处理与监测。

Result: 在真实世界心血管数据集上验证，框架展现出更高的准确率、更低的假阳性率以及更强的预测一致性，优于传统方法，在早期风险评估和临床决策支持方面表现高效。

Conclusion: 结合传统机器学习与深度学习范式的框架能够有效实现主动式医疗管理和改善患者预后，具备在心血管疾病早期诊断中的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This study introduces an intelligent framework that integrates machine learning and deep neural network ensemble techniques for early detection and prognosis of cardiovascular diseases. The system utilizes real-time physiological data collected from Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) devices, including ECG sensors, heart rate monitors, and blood pressure trackers. To ensure the accuracy and reliability of input data, preprocessing steps such as noise reduction, normalization, and missing value imputation are employed. The most significant health indicators are identified through effective feature selection methods and then processed using optimized classifiers such as Support Vector Machines (SVM), Random Forests, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), which are combined in an ensemble architecture to improve diagnostic precision. The framework demonstrates remarkable performance in predicting cardiovascular disease risk, achieving higher accuracy, reduced false positives, and enhanced consistency compared to conventional methods. It is designed on a cloud-based infrastructure that ensures scalability and real-time processing for continuous patient monitoring. Experimental evaluation on real-world cardiovascular datasets confirms the framework's efficiency in early-stage risk assessment and clinical decision support. The results highlight the potential of combining traditional machine learning and deep learning paradigms to achieve proactive healthcare management and improve patient outcomes.

</details>


### [306] [Large Language Models for HVAC Operations in Building Energy Systems: A Critical Review of Methods, Applications, and Deployment Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05314)
*Alexander Neubauer,Tianzhen Hong,Han Li,Mengbo Yu,Amin Darbandi,Yannick Fürst,Martin Kriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本系统综述分析了2023年至2026年3月间66篇关于大语言模型（LLM）用于HVAC（暖通空调）运行的同行评审研究。研究将文献按五个应用族和三个LLM方法族分类，评估证据真实性、部署就绪度及责任边界。结果显示：文献集中在建筑能耗建模（32/66篇），仅4项研究达到试点级证据，无一报告持续运营部署；没有研究被分类为"就绪可用"，3项为近期可用，63项仅为研究性质。LLM当前更适合作为语义和工作流层（如点名称标准化、文档支持的运维支持），而非自主HVAC控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化系统虽产生丰富的传感器数据，但因异构点命名、元数据缺失和文档碎片化等问题，难以从数据中获取运维洞见。作者希望通过系统梳理LLM在HVAC运维中的研究现状，评估其真实潜力与局限，为未来研究方向提供明确指引。

Method: 对66篇2023年至2026年3月发表的同行评审论文进行系统性文献综述和编码分析。每篇研究按五个应用族和三个LLM方法族进行分类，并从证据真实性、部署就绪度、以及LLM与物理HVAC决策之间的责任边界三个维度进行评估。

Result: 文献高度集中于建筑能耗建模（32/66篇）；仅4项研究达到试点级证据，没有任何研究报告持续的运营部署；无研究被认定为业界可即时采用，3项为近期待用，63项仍处于研究阶段。点名称标准化、文档支持的运维支持、BEM工作流辅助和基于物理控制器的咨询界面等受限的人机协同用例值得近期试验。

Conclusion: 当前证据支持LLM主要作为语义层和工作流层而非自主HVAC控制器。传统ML、MPC、RL和基于本体的工具在高速控制、短期数值预测和明确的映射任务上仍更成熟。未来研究应优先开展现场验证基准、运营约束下的编排评估，以及具有有限延迟和可验证安全属性的LLM-MPC/RL架构。

Abstract: Building automation systems generate rich sensor data yet remain insight-poor because heterogeneous point naming, missing metadata, and fragmented documentation obstruct their operational use. This systematic review analyses and codes 66 peer-reviewed studies on large language models (LLMs) for HVAC operations published between 2023 and March 2026. Each study is classified across five application families and three LLM method families and assessed for evidence realism, deployment readiness, and the responsibility boundary between the LLM and physical HVAC decisions. The corpus is concentrated in building energy modelling (BEM, 32 of 66 papers), while load forecasting remains too sparse for subfield-level conclusions. Only four studies reach pilot-level evidence, and none reports sustained operational deployment. No study was classified as ready-now for industry adoption; three were near-term and 63 research-only. Nevertheless, several bounded, human-in-the-loop uses merit near-term trials, including point-name normalisation, document-grounded operator support, BEM workflow assistance, and advisory interfaces around physics-based controllers. Conventional machine learning (ML), model predictive control (MPC), reinforcement learning (RL) and ontology-based tools remain more adopted for high-frequency control, short-horizon numerical forecasting, and well-posed ontology mapping, while autonomous agentic operation and unvalidated occupant proxies remain research-stage. Current evidence therefore supports LLMs primarily as semantic and workflow layers rather than autonomous HVAC controllers. Future work should prioritise field-validated benchmarks, orchestration evaluation under operational constraints, and LLM-MPC/RL architectures with bounded latency and verifiable safety properties.

</details>


### [307] [The Mirror Agent Model: a Bayesian Architecture for Interpretable Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05190)
*Michele Persiani,Thomas Hellström*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Mirror Agent Model的架构，通过将观察者模型定义为智能体模型的镜像，来生成可解释的行为和解释。该架构首先整合了以往关于智能体意图信息沟通和可读行为生成的研究成果，随后引入现成的显著性方法赋予其生成解释的新能力，并提供了初步的定性实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决智能体行为与解释的可理解性问题。传统智能体系统生成的行为往往难以被人类观察者理解，本文希望通过一种新颖的架构（Mirror Agent Model）使智能体行为具备可解释性，并能让智能体主动生成解释以促进人机之间的有效沟通。

Method: 本文构建了Mirror Agent Model架构，其核心思想是将观察者模型设计为智能体自身模型的镜像，从而使智能体能够预测和理解观察者的认知状态。论文首先回顾了在智能体意图信息沟通和可读行为生成方面的先前相关工作，然后在此基础上，利用现成的显著性方法（off-the-shelf saliency methods）为架构扩展了生成解释的能力，并进行了初步的定性评估。

Result: 论文提供了初步的定性结果，展示了Mirror Agent Model架构通过显著性方法生成解释的可行性。不过这些结果仍处于初步阶段，论文更多是概念性说明和架构演示。

Conclusion: Mirror Agent Model是一种能够同时生成可解释行为和解释的新颖架构，其通过镜像观察者模型的方式从根本上加强了智能体与观察者之间的沟通基础。显著性方法的引入为解释能力提供了补充，未来工作将可能围绕更系统的定量评估和更完善的解释生成机制展开。

Abstract: In this paper we illustrate a novel architecture generating interpretable behavior and explanations. We refer to this architecture as the Mirror Agent Model because it defines the observer model, that is the target of explicit and implicit communications, as a mirror of the agent's. With the goal of providing a general understanding of this work, we firstly show prior relevant results addressing the informative communication of agents intentions and the production of legible behavior. In the second part of the paper we furnish the architecture with novel capabilities for explanations through off-the-shelf saliency methods, followed by preliminary qualitative results.

</details>


### [308] [Technical Manual for a Toolkit for Measuring Contextual Individuation in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05333)
*José Luciano Verçosa Marques,Frederico Jorge Heitmann,Daniel Omar Perez,Marcelo Vinicius de Paula,Tárcio André dos Santos Barros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文档介绍了一个开源工具包，该工具包围绕"桥梁形式"（bridge form）这一概念构建——即同一个书写词在不同领域中以不同语义重复出现——用于干净地测试Transformer语言模型是否能通过上下文在后续层中区分同一词形的不同语义。论文详细描述了从词形规范、语料获取、表示提取到分离度测量的完整流水线，并指出每个设计选择所避免的特定方法学缺陷。此为方法论参考文档，不报告实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型的嵌入层为每个词类型分配一个单一且与上下文无关的向量，但普遍认为模型在更高层通过上下文来区分该词的不同出现。要干净地检验这一信念，需要一种构造：保持词形不变，同时其上下文和预期语义以受控且带标签的方式变化。作者提出的"桥梁形式"正是为了实现这一受控检验而设计的。

Method: 论文定义了一个完整的流水线：(1) 声明式规范桥梁形式及其来源领域；(2) 从Wikipedia获取语料；(3) 定位词的出现；(4) 按层提取表示；(5) 使用领域配对的分隔度量（silhouette测量）评估模型表示空间中的语义分离；(6) 配对的降维可视化协议。每个设计选择均对应一个需要避免的方法学陷阱。

Result: 本文不报告或解释在任何特定模型或桥梁形式集合上运行工具包的实证结果。它提供的是方法论和实施参考；工具包及其完整源码和语料以持久标识符单独存档，供后续研究中作为仪器引用。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可复现且经过方法论论证的实验基础设施，使研究者能够以严格受控的方式检验Transformer模型上下文感知词义区分能力的假设。将工具包作为可引用仪器存档，支持未来实证研究的标准化与相互可比性。

Abstract: A transformer language model assigns a single, context-independent vector to a word type at its embedding layer, yet is widely believed to individuate that word's occurrences by context in its later layers. Testing this belief cleanly requires a construct that holds the word form fixed while its context and intended sense vary in a controlled, labeled way. This manual documents an open toolkit built around such a construct, which we call a bridge form: a single written word that recurs, unchanged, across two or more subject domains with a different sense in each. We describe, and justify, every stage of the pipeline: the declarative specification of bridge forms and their source domains, corpus acquisition from Wikipedia, occurrence localization, layer-wise representation extraction, a domain-pairwise silhouette measurement of separation in the model's representation space, and a paired visualization protocol. Each design choice is presented together with the methodological failure mode it is meant to avoid (sense contamination from overly broad category labels, the multi-group bias of the silhouette coefficient, subword-tokenization misalignment, and axis-comparability artifacts in dimensionality-reduced plots, among others). This manuscript is a methodological and implementation reference: it does not report or interpret empirical outcomes of running the toolkit on any particular model or bridge-form set. The toolkit, its full source, and the corpora used to exercise it are archived separately (Section 9) under a persistent identifier, and are intended to be cited as an instrument by studies that use it to produce and interpret empirical results.

</details>


### [309] [What Matters in On-Policy Distillation? A Perspective on Data Efficiency and Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05198)
*Zhinan Hou,Jiaqi Zhang,Xunliang Cai,Keyou You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对On-Policy Distillation (OPD)中的数据效率和选择机制进行了实证研究。研究发现1-shot OPD（仅用一个例子训练）在推理领域普遍有效，且困难示例往往带来更大性能提升。更进一步的分析表明，学生模型的提升并非由高词元熵驱动，而是由困难问题自然产生的更长CoT（思维链）路径驱动，这些长CoT有助于学生与教师保持更长期的对齐，并学习到短CoT中缺失的批判性思维模式（如反思）。基于此，作者提出仅选择困难示例（甚至包含教师完全无法解决的"不可解"示例）的简单数据选择方法，实验表明仅用8个精选困难示例训练即可匹配17K数据集的基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD已成为增强大语言模型推理能力的广泛采用的后训练范式，但其数据中心的机制（数据效率和数据选择）尚未被充分探索。理解哪些训练数据真正驱动学生模型的提升，以及如何高效选择数据，对于提升OPD的效率和效果具有重要意义。

Method: 作者首先在极端设置下（仅用一个示例训练，即1-shot OPD）进行系统实证；然后通过分析揭示学生模型提升的真正驱动因素——较长CoT路径而非高词元熵；最后提出一个简单但有效的基于困难度（hardness）的数据选择方法，该方法甚至可以利用教师无法解决的"不可解"示例。

Result: 1) 1-shot OPD在所有采样训练示例上均一致有效，且更难示例往往带来更大性能提升；2) 学生模型的提升主要归因于长CoT路径（而非高词元熵），长CoT可维持与教师的长期对齐并学习反思等批判性思维模式；3) 仅用8个精选困难示例训练学生模型即可匹配17K数据集基线的性能，在1.5B至7B的四种模型上均得到验证。

Conclusion: OPD中数据质量远比数据数量重要，通过简单的困难度选择即可实现极端的训练数据压缩。这一发现挑战了大规模数据驱动的后训练范式，为OPD的高效实践提供了新思路，表明"少而难"的数据选择策略可能优于"多而全"的数据集。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has emerged as a widely adopted post-training paradigm for enhancing large language models in reasoning domains. However, the data-centric mechanisms in OPD remain relatively underexplored. This paper presents a empirical study of data efficiency and data selection in OPD. We begin by investigating an extreme setting: training OPD on only one example, namely 1-shot OPD. Surprisingly, we find that 1-shot OPD is consistently effective across all sampled training examples and harder examples often yield superior performance gain. We next investigate what actually drives the student model's improvement in the training data. Our analysis reveals that the improvement is not driven by high token entropy, but the longer CoT paths which hard problems naturally generate. Training on longer CoT can help maintain closer alignment with the teacher over a long reasoning horizon, and learn critical thinking patterns usually missing in short CoTs, such as reflection (e.g., ``Alternatively''). Based on these insights, we propose a simple data selection method that selects only hard examples for training, where even ``unsolvable'' examples that completely exceed the teacher's capability can be successfully used. Our experiments conducted on four models ranging from 1.5B to 7B show that training the student model on only 8 selected hard examples matches the performance of the 17K dataset baseline.

</details>


### [310] [CABAL: Multi-Agent Simulacra for Tracing the Effects of Collusive Bidding in Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05227)
*Jicheng Zhou,Kemou Li,Kahim Wong,Zheyuan Li,Zhuan Shi,Fengpeng Li,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端多智能体模拟框架，用于研究审稿人共谋投标对评审分配完整性的影响，通过控制会议环境并配置LLM驱动的审稿人智能体来对比诚实与共谋策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将投标、审稿人分配和评审操纵视为独立阶段，缺乏对共谋投标生命周期效应的整体理解；同时真实世界中共谋意图难以观测，且缺少同一会议的反事实数据，阻碍了相关实证分析。

Method: 构建多智能体模拟框架，将会议环境固定，用LLM驱动审稿人智能体并配置诚实或共谋策略；提出基于亲和力引导的共谋投标策略，利用审稿人-论文的互惠亲和力构造共谋环并选择目标论文，产生专业知识一致的攻击而非任意目标攻击。

Result: 受控实验表明共谋投标使目标论文捕获率翻倍以上；被分配到的共谋者给目标论文的评分比诚实合审人高约两分，而会议整体影响相对有限；现有投标阶段检测器仅提供有限的共谋证据——固定三元组检测器压力测试中，天然的正向投标图与良性亲和力混淆，而仅使用Very-High诊断视图可实现精确但覆盖率低的局部恢复。

Conclusion: 研究表明共谋投标对个别目标论文有显著威胁，但对会议全局影响有限；现有检测方法难以有效识别共谋，需要更精细的诊断视角来提升检测覆盖率。

Abstract: Recent reports during the AAAI-27 review cycle highlight the risk of reviewers coordinating bids for reciprocal assignment advantage. Prior work treats bidding, reviewer assignment, and review manipulation as separate stages, leaving the lifecycle effects of collusive bidding unclear. Real-world analysis is further constrained by typically unobservable collusive intent and the lack of counterfactuals for the same conference. Motivated by this gap, we introduce \alg, an end-to-end multi-agent simulacra framework for studying reviewer assignment integrity by holding the conference environment fixed and configuring LLM-driven reviewer agents with honest or collusive policies. We further develop an affinity-guided collusive bidding strategy that uses mutual reviewer-paper affinities to construct collusion rings and select target papers, producing expertise-consistent rather than arbitrarily targeted attacks. Controlled experiments show that collusive bidding more than doubles target-paper capture and that assigned colluders score target papers about two points higher than honest co-reviewers, while conference-wide effects remain comparatively modest. Evaluated bid-phase detectors provide only limited evidence of collusion: in a fixed-triplet detector stress test, native positive-bid graphs are confounded by benign affinity, while a Very-High-only diagnostic view enables precise but low-coverage local recovery.

</details>


### [311] [Multi-Step Tool-Calling over Korean Open Public APIs: A Benchmark and a Data-Synthesis Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05395)
*Dain Kim,Eungi Cho,Kyumin Kim,Shinyeong Noh,Kyuseong Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KOPA-Bench基准（145个真实世界韩国公共API任务）和EDGE方法（基于实时执行验证的工具调用数据合成框架），通过构建并验证工具间输出-输入链接图来合成可执行的多步骤轨迹，使用GRPO对9B模型微调后几乎达到27B模型性能，并在KOPA-Bench和BFCL上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据主权法规要求公共机构部署开源、本地化的LLM智能体，需在实时政府API间链式调用多个工具。然而开源模型在多步骤场景下表现不佳，且缺乏衡量这一差距的基准。

Method: 构建KOPA-Bench基准（145个真实任务）；提出EDGE方法：构建工具间输出到输入的链接图，仅保留在实时API调用中验证成功的链接，遍历这些已验证链接合成可执行的多步骤轨迹，再用GRPO对模型进行微调。

Result: 经EDGE合成数据微调的9B模型几乎媲美未微调的27B模型（同模型家族），不仅在KOPA-Bench上显著提升，在BFCL基准上也表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过实时执行验证的数据合成能有效缩小开源小模型与更大模型在多步骤工具调用场景下的性能差距，且该方法具有跨基准的泛化能力。

Abstract: Data-sovereignty regulations increasingly require public institutions to deploy open-source, on-premise LLM agents that chain multiple tool-calls across live government APIs. However, open-source models consistently underperform in this multi-step setting, and no existing benchmark measures the gap. We introduce the Korean Open Public API Benchmark (KOPA-Bench), comprising 145 real-world tasks. To close this gap, we present EDGE, an Execution-grounded Dynamic Graph for tool-calling data synthEsis driven by live execution. EDGE builds a graph of how each tool's output can feed another's input, keeps only the links that succeed when actually called against the live APIs, and traverses these verified links to synthesize executable multi-step trajectories. Fine-tuned via GRPO on the resulting dataset, our 9B model nearly matches the untuned 27B model from the same family, improving substantially not only on KOPA-Bench but also on the BFCL benchmark.

</details>


### [312] [ACE: Adaptive Calibration-Free Expert Skipping for MoE-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05228)
*Zukang Xu,Zhixiong Zhao,Xing Hu,Jiangyong Yu,Houji Wen,Jun Li,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE框架，一种无需训练、无需校准数据且保持模型检查点不变的自适应专家跳过方法，用于MoE大语言模型。ACE通过全局频谱代理（GSP）和路由器条件精炼（RCR）两个互补组件来估计每个被路由专家的实际贡献，在推理时仅在两个视角都判定某专家为低贡献时才跳过它，同时始终保留top-1专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE架构中固定top-k路由对每个token激活相同数量的专家槽位，导致大量冗余计算。已有的专家跳过方法依赖路由置信度、校准数据或额外训练，无法可靠估计被路由专家的实际贡献。

Method: ACE包含两个互补组件：1) 全局频谱代理（GSP），结合gate、up、down投影及RMSNorm缩放估计全局变换能力；2) 路由器条件精炼（RCR），从中心化路由器权重构建专家特定方向原型，并沿路由偏好方向评估专家响应。推理时结合运行时路由门控两者的估计，仅当两个视角都判定专家为低贡献时跳过，始终保留top-1专家。所有统计量离线计算，在线仅需查表和轻量标量运算。

Result: 在三个MoE大语言模型和八个基准上的实验表明，ACE一致优于现有静态和动态基线，在激进专家跳过场景下优势更加明显。例如在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上以50%跳过率，ACE将WikiText-2困惑度降低7.96%，平均下游准确率比最强竞争方法提高4.15个百分点。

Conclusion: ACE通过双视角互补估计实现了更可靠的专家贡献评估，在无需训练和校准数据的情况下有效减少MoE推理中的冗余计算，且优势在激进跳过比率下更加显著。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures provide an efficient paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs), yet fixed top-k routing activates the same number of expert slots for every token, causing substantial redundant computation. Existing expert-skipping methods often rely on router confidence, calibration data, or additional training, and therefore cannot reliably estimate the actual contribution of routed experts. To this end, we propose ACE, a training-free, calibration-free, and checkpoint-preserving framework for token-adaptive expert skipping in MoE-based LLMs. ACE contains two complementary components: 1) Global Spectral Proxy (GSP), which estimates global transformation capacity from the coupled gate, up, and down projections together with RMSNorm scaling; and 2) Router-Conditioned Refinement (RCR), which constructs expert-specific direction prototypes from centered router weights and evaluates expert responses along routing-preferred directions. During inference, ACE combines both estimates with runtime router gates and skips an expert slot only when both views identify it as low-contribution, while always retaining the top-1 expert. All expert statistics are computed offline, leaving only table lookups and lightweight scalar operations online. Extensive experiments across three MoE-based LLMs and eight benchmarks demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms existing static and dynamic baselines, with increasingly pronounced advantages under aggressive expert skipping. For instance, at a 50% skipping ratio on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, ACE reduces WikiText-2 perplexity by 7.96% and improves average downstream accuracy by 4.15 percentage points over the strongest competing method.

</details>


### [313] [Substrate-Aware AI Agents: Execution Context as a First-Class Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05232)
*Manu Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"基底盲区"(substrate blindness)概念——自主AI智能体在规划时缺少执行环境上下文(如内存、运行时间、计算约束)。通过在数值代码生成任务中进行受控实验，向Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-Sol和Gemini 3.7 Flash披露128MB RAM和10秒执行合约后，生成的代码在峰值内存和墙钟时间上显著改善，执行速度最高提升3.1倍；在更严格的96MB合约下，正确且不超预算的成功率大幅提升，峰值内存降低49-74%，墙钟时间降低35-64%。这证明了"基底感知智能体规划"的受控概念验证：简单的执行合约能诱导程序产生主动的结构性适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体在选择行动时，其环境的内存、执行时间、运行时和计算约束决定了什么才是合适的计划，但现有智能体规划通常将这些执行上下文排除在外（称为"基底盲区"）。作者希望通过可观察的数值代码生成任务来测试这一命题，探究向模型提供明确执行约束是否能诱导其生成更符合资源预算的代码。

Method: 选取三个前沿模型（Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-Sol、Gemini 3.7 Flash）进行高维成对欧氏距离任务的代码生成。实验分为两组：仅给定任务描述（task-only）与任务描述加128MB RAM和10秒墙钟执行合约（contract-disclosed）。随后测量生成代码的峰值进程内存和墙钟时间，进行14次可执行索引对齐比较；并在96MB更紧合约下进行更严格测试，每个模型独立采样5次。

Result: 在128MB合约下，14次可比较中13次披露合约后峰值内存降低，所有三个队列的平均墙钟时间均减少，执行最快提升3.1倍。结构化代码变化包括有界阻塞、float32保留、上三角遍历和原地/内存映射缓冲区。在96MB合约下，Claude Opus 5达到4/5、GPT-5.6-Sol达到5/5、Gemini 3.7 Flash达到3/5的正确且在预算内成功率，远超task-only的0/5、1/5和0/5；队列平均最大驻留集大小(RSS)降低49-74%，墙钟时间降低35-64%。

Conclusion: 研究建立了基底感知智能体规划的受控概念验证：即使是最简的执行合约声明也能促使生成程序产生主动的结构性适应，将计算从不加约束的分配转移开来，在代码执行前就显著改善资源-时间行为。这表明将执行上下文纳入智能体规划状态是提升AI代理实际部署效能的重要方向。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly select actions in environments whose memory, execution-time, runtime, compute, and operational constraints determine what counts as a suitable plan. We call the absence of this execution context from an agent's planning state substrate blindness. We test this general proposition through numerical code generation, where selected implementation choices and operational consequences are directly observable. Three frontier model configurations--Anthropic Claude Opus 5, OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol, and Google Gemini 3.7 Flash--generate code for a high-dimensional pairwise Euclidean-distance task either from the task alone or with a 128 MB RAM and 10.0 s wall-time contract. Contract disclosure reduced measured peak process memory in 13 of 14 executable index-aligned task-only versus contract-disclosed comparisons and reduced mean wall time in all three cohorts, making execution up to 3.1x faster. Across the audited corpus, disclosure produced structural code changes including bounded blocking, float32 retention, upper-triangle traversal, and in-place or memory-mapped buffers. At a tighter 96 MB contract, independently sampled contract-disclosed cohorts achieved correct-and-within-budget outcomes of 4/5 for Claude Opus 5, 5/5 for GPT-5.6-Sol, and 3/5 for Gemini 3.7 Flash, compared with task-only outcomes of 0/5, 1/5, and 0/5; cohort mean MaxRSS and wall time were 49-74% and 35-64% lower than their task-only references. These results establish a controlled proof of concept for substrate-aware agent planning: a minimal execution contract induces proactive structural adaptation in generated programs, shifting computation away from unconstrained allocations and substantially improving observed resource-time profiles before execution.

</details>


### [314] [Uncensored Open-weight Models: Redistribution as the Persistence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05241)
*10a Labs,:,Juliette Garcia,Hailey May,Bobby McKenzie,David Pham,Matthew Swain,Joshua Valdez,Corie Wieland,Zachary Yahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文描绘了一个快速扩张的生态系统，该生态系统正在移除开源AI模型的内置安全防护。研究识别了关键生产者、下游复制品和新兴应用，发现2024年1月至2026年3月期间HuggingFace上有3,471个原始无审查模型，平均被重新打包2.4次；量化压缩后的模型在多个平台间持久存在且更易部署。在1,643个GitHub应用中，25%被归类为明确恶意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无审查开源AI模型生态系统的规模、结构和动态，揭示安全护栏被移除后的模型如何扩散、持久化并被恶意利用。

Method: 通过大规模数据挖掘和分析，对HuggingFace、Ollama和GitHub等平台进行系统性的生态系统画像，识别生产者、复制品和应用进行分类统计。

Result: 识别出3,471个原始无审查模型和8,164个压缩再分发版本；三个主要参与者占所有再分发的52%；在1,643个GitHub应用中，25%为明确恶意用途。

Conclusion: 无审查模型的去中心化复制和量化机制使其在上游删除后仍能持久存在，且恶意应用比例显著，对AI安全治理构成严峻挑战，需要关注开源模型生态的缓解策略。

Abstract: A rapidly expanding ecosystem of actors is removing built-in safety guardrails from open-weight AI models. We profile this ecosystem by identifying key producers, downstream reproductions, and emerging applications. Between January 2024 and March 2026, we identified 3,471 original uncensored models on HuggingFace, each repackaged an average of 2.4 times; three actors account for 52% of all 8,164 compressed redistributions. Once quantized and mirrored across separate accounts, formats, and registries such as Ollama, these models persist regardless of upstream removal and become easier to deploy downstream. Of the 1,643 identified GitHub applications integrating uncensored large language models (ULLMs), 25% were classified as explicitly malicious.

</details>


### [315] [Do LLMs Exhibit Coherent Knowledge Structures in Mathematical Reasoning? A Perspective from Knowledge Space Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05245)
*Peng Cui,Heejin Do,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于知识空间理论（KST）评估LLM在数学推理中的知识结构，发现LLM不遵循人类知识依赖关系，且不同LLM之间知识结构一致性低，这些缺陷在准确率评估中难以显现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类知识具有层次依赖结构（需先掌握前置概念才能掌握后续概念），而当前LLM虽在复杂推理上表现优异，但尚不清楚其是否具备类似人类的结构化、连贯的知识组织方式。现有评估主要关注准确率，缺乏对知识结构本身规范性（是否遵循知识依赖关系）的考察。

Method: 以知识空间理论（KST）为规范框架，构建评估LLM在数学推理中知识结构的方法论：将知识前置关系作为判定标准，对8个开源和闭源LLM进行测试，并与真实人类学习者的知识结构进行比较分析。同时评估依赖关系的违反情况、上下文相关知识的利用能力，以及不同模型间知识结构的一致性。

Result: （1）LLM不遵循人类知识结构：频繁违反知识依赖关系，未能利用上下文中相关知识提升依赖题目的表现；（2）不同LLM之间知识结构不一致，知识分布重叠度低；（3）这些结构性缺陷在基于准确率和LLM-as-judge的评估中基本不可见。

Conclusion: 当前LLM的知识不遵循人类类似的结构化组织，其推理能力强可能来自非结构化的统计模式而非有组织的知识体系，这一发现对如何评估和改进LLM的能力提出了新的视角和要求。

Abstract: Human knowledge is inherently structured and interdependent: mastery of a concept requires prior mastery of its prerequisites, a principle formalized by Knowledge Space Theory (KST). While LLMs achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they exhibit coherent, human-like knowledge structure. We introduce a KST-grounded framework for evaluating LLM knowledge structure in mathematical reasoning, using it as a normative framework to analyze whether LLM behavior adheres to principled knowledge dependencies. Evaluating eight open- and closed-source LLMs against real human learners, we find that (1) LLMs do not adhere to human knowledge structure -- they frequently violate knowledge dependencies and fail to leverage related knowledge provided in context to improve performance on dependent questions; (2) LLMs do not share a consistent knowledge structure among themselves, as reflected by low overlap in their knowledge distributions. Furthermore, these structural deficiencies remain largely invisible to accuracy-based and LLM-as-judge evaluations. Together, our results provide behavioral evidence that current LLMs knowledge does not follow a human-like structure.

</details>


### [316] [A Unified Physics-Aware Quantum Machine Learning Framework across Power GaN HEMTs and Logic Nanowire FETs: Predicting Unseen Process Splits and Held-Out Geometry Combinations with Lower Error and Tighter Split-to-Split Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05251)
*Rushat Rai,Yun-Yuan Wang,Autsada Kakaen,Pei-Jie Chang,Doan Viet Nguyen,Yuan-Chieh Chiu,Doldet Tantraviwat,Niall Tumilty,Simon See,Wen-Jay Lee,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Tian-Li Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的强化学习框架，利用图神经网络策略结合近端策略优化（PPO）自动搜索紧凑参数化量子电路（PQC），用于数据稀缺的器件建模。该框架在11个目标上均优于6个经典基线，在HEMT和NWFET器件上分别实现高达59%-84%的误差降低和81%-82%的折叠变异性改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统器件建模在数据稀缺场景下表现不佳，而手动设计量子电路或依赖领域专家知识的方法难以泛化且效率低下。作者希望在不施加显式物理约束或器件特定方程的前提下，自动发现紧凑且兼具低分布外（OOD）误差与物理一致性的代理模型。

Method: 采用统一的强化学习框架：1) 图神经网络（GNN）作为策略网络，通过PPO算法优化；2) 以留一组交叉验证（LOGOCV）误差作为奖励信号，在held-out工艺或几何分组上评估；3) 策略自动搜索PQC电路架构，作为数据稀缺器件建模的紧凑替代模型。

Result: 在11个目标上均达到最低平均绝对误差（MAE），优于6种经典基线。HEMT器件上Ioff误差降低59%，VTH折叠变异性收窄81%；NWFET器件上VTH/SS/Ioff误差降低84%，Ioff折叠变异性收窄82%。

Conclusion: RL自动选择的、经典模拟的PQC可作为数据稀缺场景下紧凑且有效的器件代理模型，具备低OOD误差和改进的物理一致性，即便未显式施加物理约束或领域方程，也能在两个评估的器件数据集上取得优异表现。

Abstract: We present a unified reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that discovers compact parametrized quantum circuits (PQCs) for data-scarce device modeling. A graph neural network (GNN) policy optimized by proximal policy optimization (PPO) searches circuit architectures using leave-one-group-out cross-validation (LOGOCV) error on held-out process or geometry groups as the reward. The framework achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) on all 11 targets versus six classical baselines, with 59% lower error (Ioff) and 81% tighter fold variability (VTH) for HEMTs and 84% lower error (VTH, SS, Ioff) and 82% tighter fold variability (Ioff) for NWFETs. These results demonstrate the potential of RL-selected, classically simulated PQCs as compact surrogates with low OOD error and improved physical consistency, despite imposing no explicit physical constraints, penalty terms, or device-specific equations, on the two evaluated device datasets.

</details>


### [317] [Commonsense Reasoning in Computer Vision: Foundations, Recent Advancements, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05257)
*Bahar Uddin Mahmud,Sumit Barua,Guan Yue Hong,Ajay Gupta,Hexu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于将常识知识整合到计算机视觉任务中的综合性综述，系统回顾了基于知识图谱、场景图、神经符号模型和常识增强Transformer的方法，并讨论了数据集偏差、知识不完备性和集成挑战等限制，最后展望了跨模态推理、可扩展常识知识注入和神经符号混合架构等未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CNN视觉模型仅能识别图像中的特定物体，缺乏对场景的整体理解和物体间关系的推理能力。将常识知识融入计算机视觉可以增强模型对日常场景的理解，实现更整体化的场景解读，从而提升对象识别、空间推理和上下文预测能力。

Method: 作为一篇综述论文，系统性地梳理和分类了当前将常识知识集成到计算机视觉任务的各类方法，包括：基于知识图谱的方法、基于场景图的方法、神经符号模型以及常识增强的Transformer架构。

Result: 系统归类了现有的常识视觉推理方法，并识别出当前的主要局限，包括数据集偏差、知识不完备性以及知识集成所面临的技术挑战。

Conclusion: 提出了三条未来研究方向：跨模态推理、可扩展的常识知识注入机制，以及神经符号混合架构，旨在推动开发真正智能的视觉系统。

Abstract: Commonsense reasoning in computer vision encompasses integrating visual data and contextual knowledge, crucial for enhancing AI's understanding of everyday scenarios. This understanding not only improves machine learning models but also enhances their ability to interact meaningfully with humans and the environment. Unlike CNN-based conventional vision models, which are designed to identify objects within a specific image, incorporating commonsense knowledge enables models to interpret scenes in a more holistic manner, thereby improving their spatial ability to reason about relationships among objects and actions. This integration not only enhances object recognition but also facilitates a deeper understanding of the contextual factors, ultimately leading to more precise predictions and interactions in real-world applications. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of recent developments that integrate commonsense knowledge into computer vision tasks. We systematically review approaches based on knowledge graphs, scene graphs, neuro-symbolic models, and commonsense-augmented transformers. We also outline current limitations related to dataset bias, knowledge incompleteness, and integration challenges. Finally, we highlight prospective research trajectories in cross-modal reasoning, scalable commonsense knowledge injection, and neuro-symbolic hybrid architectures to develop truly intelligent visual systems.

</details>


### [318] [AI for Computational Design Science: A Responsible Human-AI Framework and Case Study on Short-Form Video Safety Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05270)
*Wenli Zhang,Jiaheng Xie,Zhihe Pan,Yidong Chai,Xiao Fang,Sudha Ram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI4CDS，一个五阶段方法论框架，用于指导AI积极参与计算设计科学研究（CDS）。该框架在扩大问题与设计搜索空间的同时，确保研究者保留领域锚定、可接受性、验证和科学判断的责任，并通过ChildRiskGuard案例（用于检测不适合儿童观看的短视频的可解释系统）进行实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AI正在深刻改变信息系统设计研究的方式，但现有文献对AI主动参与问题定义、资源构建、设计搜索、评估和知识抽象时的计算设计科学（CDS）提供的方法论指导十分有限，缺乏系统性的责任划分与协作治理框架。

Method: 提出AI4CDS五阶段方法论框架，以渐进信任、可逆性、可审计性和差异化可复现性为协作治理原则。通过ChildRiskGuard案例进行实例化——该可解释系统用于检测不适合儿童的短视频，将受众依赖的安全性和解释忠实度转化为三个技术挑战，构建了区分通用与儿童特定风险、表征不同发展阶段风险机制、并将概念级解释纳入预测计算的构件。

Result: ChildRiskGuard在检测不适合儿童的短视频任务中取得了0.769的F1分数，显著优于直接应用通用内容安全模型，同时与强基准模型保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于AI4CDS作为负责任的AI赋能CDS框架；ChildRiskGuard则提供了过程与构件证据，展示AI扩展、研究者治理的设计方式如何生成和评估新的计算设计知识。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is transforming not only what information systems researchers design, but also how design research is conducted. Yet existing literature offers limited guidance for computational design science (CDS) when AI actively participates in problem formulation, resource construction, design search, evaluation, and knowledge abstraction. We develop AI for Computational Design Science (AI4CDS), a five-phase methodological framework in which AI expands problem and design search while researchers retain responsibility for domain grounding, admissibility, verification, and scientific judgment. Collaboration is governed by graduated trust, reversibility, auditability, and differentiated reproducibility. We instantiate AI4CDS through ChildRiskGuard, an interpretable artifact for detecting short-form videos inappropriate for children, while documenting AI interactions, rejected alternatives, corrections, and audit trails. The case translates audience-dependent safety and explanation faithfulness into three technical challenges and develops an artifact that separates generic from child-specific risk, represents distinct developmental-risk mechanisms, and makes concept-level explanations part of the predictive computation. ChildRiskGuard achieves an F1 score of 0.769, substantially outperforming direct application of a general-purpose content-safety model while remaining competitive with strong benchmarks. The primary contribution is AI4CDS as a responsible framework for AI-enabled CDS; ChildRiskGuard provides process and artifact evidence of how AI-expanded, researcher-governed design can generate and evaluate novel computational design knowledge.

</details>


### [319] [Don't Drop Dropout: Optimizing Layer Sparsity for Efficient LLM Training and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05275)
*Mostafa Elhoushi,Alex Pretko,Nolan Dey,Bin Claire Zhang,Gavia Gray,Gurpreet Gosal,Abdulrahman Mahmoud,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了层丢失（layer dropout）在大语言模型预训练中的作用，证明在最优的层分布、时间调度和优化器超参数下，层丢失可以在相同FLOPs下降低损失，节省高达25%的训练FLOPs，并在推理阶段通过早停、中间层跳过和自推测解码实现最高1.5倍加速，实验覆盖271M到8.2B参数的模型和高达160B token的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管层丢失在语言和视觉Transformer中已被证明能加速训练、提升精度并对零样本层剪枝具有鲁棒性，但随着模型和数据集规模的扩大，层丢失在大语言模型预训练recipe中基本消失。已有工作仅报告dropout可能降低精度，但缺乏系统性研究来定量评估并缓解这一影响。

Method: 作者通过2400多次训练实验，系统探索了层丢失的最优层分布、时间调度（time schedule）和优化器超参数配置，并在从271M到8.2B参数规模的模型以及高达160B token的数据集上进行验证，所有预训练实验在Cerebras CS-3系统上完成。

Result: 1) 在相同训练FLOPs下，采用层丢失可降低损失；2) 在给定训练步数下，可节省高达25%的训练FLOPs，同时保持相当或更低的验证损失；3) 层丢失支持早停、中间层跳过和自推测解码等后训练优化，实现最高1.5倍推理加速且精度损失可忽略；4) 上述结论在2400多次实验中，从271M到8.2B参数规模均可靠复现。

Conclusion: 层丢失应当被纳入最先进的大语言模型训练流程中，这一方法在训练效率和推理优化方面均具有显著收益，且其优势能够可靠地扩展到大规模训练场景。

Abstract: Layer dropout (a.k.a. stochastic depth) has been shown to enable faster training, higher accuracy, and robustness to zero-shot layer pruning in both language and vision transformers. However, as models and datasets have scaled, dropout - particularly layer dropout - has largely disappeared from large language models (LLMs) pre-training recipes. While some prior work has reported that dropout can degrade accuracy, no comprehensive study has quantified, let alone mitigated, this effect. In this study, we show that layer dropout should be used in state-of-the-art LLM training, establishing best practices and scaling analysis for both training and post-training benefits. Concretely, with optimal layer distribution, time schedule, and optimizer hyperparameters, we observe that at the same training FLOPs layer dropout leads to lower loss. For a given number of training steps, LLMs can achieve lower or similar validation loss while saving upto 25% of training FLOPs. Moreover, layer dropout enables significant post-training optimizations, such as early exit, intermediate-layer skipping, and self-speculative decoding, yielding up to 1.5x inference speedup with negligible accuracy loss. Across more than 2400 training experiments, spanning models from 271M to 8.2B parameters and datasets up to 160B tokens, we demonstrate that these findings extend reliably to large-scale training regimes. All pre-training experiments were run on Cerebras CS-3 systems.

</details>


### [320] [Testing Interchangeability in LLM Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05279)
*Jianxin Gao,Tianyi Yu,Linna Deng,Runze Li,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究检验了生产型多智能体系统中"智能体可互换"的假设。通过在相同任务上独立形成8支队伍、交换角色匹配的智能体后发现：换人虽然对任务分数影响很小，但会显著增加通信开销（16%-63%），并且在Hanabi中换入的智能体比新手更昂贵。这一代价与智能体与其前搭档形成的惯例干扰一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产型多智能体系统通常假设填充某一角色的智能体是可互换的，即任何能胜任该工作的智能体都可以随时替代。作者想要检验这一假设是否成立，因为如果智能体之间形成了隐性的协作惯例，那么简单的角色替换可能会破坏系统协调效率。

Method: 在相同任务上从同一基础模型独立形成8支队伍，每支队伍中的智能体在10次组建回合中维护私人笔记本。然后交换角色匹配的智能体，在留出任务上测量变化。使用安慰剂（仅复制换人造成的干扰而不真正更换人员）作为对照。通过三个消融实验（基础模型、解码温度、组建时长）检验影响因素。

Result: 换人几乎不影响任务分数，但通信成本增加16%-63%。在Hanabi中，换入的智能体比新手更昂贵，表明与旧搭档形成的惯例存在干扰。在Collab-Overcooked中，当设定议程的智能体被替换时，额外的通信大部分来自留下的智能体。交换代价与独立形成队伍之间的漂移程度相关：贪婪解码降低两者，而加倍队伍历史提升两者。

Conclusion: 在这些设置中，智能体在任务结果上比在协调效率上更可替代。较长的组建历史会产生更大的交换效应，说明惯例的固化程度是影响智能体可互换性的关键因素。

Abstract: Production multi-agent systems replace agents constantly, on the assumption that an agent filling a role is interchangeable with any other agent that can do the job. We test that assumption. Eight teams per setting are formed independently from one base model on the same tasks, each agent keeping a private notebook across ten formation episodes; we then trade role-matched agents between teams and measure what changes on held-out tasks. Against a placebo that reproduces the disruption of a roster change without changing who occupies the seat, a swap costs little in task score but raises the communication a team spends per unit of progress by 16 to 63 percent, and in Hanabi a swapped agent is more expensive than an inexperienced one, consistent with interference from conventions learned with its former partner. In Collab-Overcooked, when the agent that sets the agenda is replaced, most of the extra communication comes from the agent that stayed. Three ablations, over base models, decoding temperature and formation length, move the swap penalty alongside one other quantity: how far independently formed teams drift apart. Greedy decoding lowers both; doubling a team's history raises both. In these settings, agents are more fungible in task outcome than in coordination efficiency, with larger swap effects after longer formation histories.

</details>


### [321] [GUT: Quantifying and Optimizing the Reasoning Uncertainty of LLMs via Graph Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05284)
*Shuang Liang,Xin-Yu Hu,Xiang-Jun Ou,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图复杂度的不确定性（GUT）方法，用于研究和降低大语言模型（LLM）推理过程中的不确定性。GUT将每种推理链的潜在分支用有向无环图（DAG）来表征，确保所有潜在分支在图中被全面覆盖，并在此基础上构建了量化（GUT-Q）和优化（GUT-O）两个模块，分别通过图复杂度近似推理空间复杂度来量化不确定性，以及将负不确定性作为强化学习中的奖励函数来实现不确定性优化。实验在四个LLM和五个数据集上验证了GUT的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理能力方面取得了显著进展，但其推理过程常表现出不确定性：即使输入相同提示，模型也会产生大量发散的分支，其中某些分支呈现明显不可靠甚至荒谬的推理链条和结果。这种不确定性严重影响了LLM推理的可靠性和可信度，因此需要系统性的方法来量化和降低这种推理不确定性。

Method: GUT方法的核心思想是利用有向无环图（DAG）来表征每条推理链的潜在分支，从而在图空间中全面覆盖所有分支。GUT包含两个核心模块：（1）GUT-Q（量化模块）：通过近似推理空间复杂度，用图复杂度来度量LLM的推理不确定性；（2）GUT-O（优化模块）：将负不确定性作为强化学习中的奖励函数，从而实现不确定性优化，降低LLM推理的不确定性。

Result: 在四个LLM和五个数据集上进行的实验验证了GUT方法的有效性，表明GUT能够准确量化LLM推理不确定性，并有效降低推理过程中的不确定性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种全新的图复杂度视角来理解和处理LLM推理不确定性的框架，GUT方法不仅在量化不确定性方面提供了新的思路，还通过强化学习机制实现了不确定性的主动优化，为提升LLM推理的可靠性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed great advances in the reasoning ability of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the reasoning processes of LLMs often exhibit uncertainty, where LLMs often produce a proliferation of divergent branches at each reasoning step even when fed the same prompting inputs, and certain branches exhibit evidently incredible, even nonsensical, reasoning chains and results. In this paper, we propose the Graph-complexity-based UncerTainty (GUT) method for investigating the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs. The key idea of GUT is to characterize the potential branches of each reasoning chain with a directed acyclic graph, thereby ensuring that all potential branches are comprehensively covered within the graph space. Building upon this recognition, we further build two modules of GUT, that is, a Quantification (GUT-Q) module and an Optimization (GUT-O) module, for quantifying and reducing the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs, respectively. GUT-Q measures LLM reasoning uncertainty by approximating the reasoning space complexity with graph complexity. GUT-O implements uncertainty optimization by treating negative uncertainty as the reward function in reinforcement learning. Experimental results conducted on four LLMs and five datasets validate the effectiveness of GUT.

</details>


### [322] [Beyond Aggregate Scores: Behavioral Correctness Assumptions for Assessing Reference-Based Automatic Evaluation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05289)
*Maria Mahbub,Ashley Rice,Michael R. Munroe,Amidu Kamara,Amir Sadovnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"行为正确性假设"（behavioral correctness assumptions）这一新框架，用于补充传统的参考式自动评估方法的元评估。通过定义保持正确性和改变正确性的假设分类，并利用受控响应转换来测试评估器的预期评分行为，系统研究了不同评估器在行为层面的表现，发现传统聚合指标无法揭示的评估器行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的元评估方法主要衡量评估器与人类判断或基准标签的一致性，这只能提供有限的洞察，无法揭示评估器在受控条件下的行为是否符合预期。需要一种补充性框架，在受控条件下系统性地检验评估器的行为正确性。

Method: 提出行为正确性假设框架，包括保持正确性和改变正确性两类假设的分类体系；通过受控响应转换（如词法、字符级、语义层面等变换）来定义具体的预期评分行为；评估多种评估器（词法级、字符级、语义级、基于LLM的和混合型），分析其在假设层面的行为、稳定性、敏感性、重复运行变异性、配置敏感性和可复现性。

Result: 实验发现：没有任何评估器能满足所有提出的正确性假设；聚合表现相似的评估器可能表现出截然不同的行为特征；不同评估范式之间存在明显的行为权衡。

Conclusion: 行为正确性假设能够提供传统聚合元评估所掩盖的诊断性信息，为参考式自动评估方法的深入理解和选择提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: Automated reference-based evaluation methods play a critical role in assessing natural language generation systems. Existing meta-evaluation primarily measures agreement with human judgments or benchmark labels, providing limited insight into evaluator behavior under controlled conditions. We introduce behavioral correctness assumptions, a complementary framework for evaluating reference-based automatic evaluation methods. We define a taxonomy of correctness-preserving and correctness-altering assumptions and operationalize them through controlled response transformations that specify expected scoring behaviors. We evaluate diverse lexical, character-level, semantic, LLM-based, and hybrid evaluators and analyze their assumption-level behavior, stability, sensitivity, repeat-run variability, configuration sensitivity, and reproducibility. Our experiments reveal distinct behavioral trade-offs across evaluation paradigms: no evaluator satisfies all proposed correctness assumptions, and evaluators with similar aggregate performance can exhibit substantially different behavioral profiles. These findings demonstrate that behavioral correctness assumptions provide diagnostic information obscured by conventional aggregate meta-evaluation.

</details>


### [323] [RISE: Recursive Improvement via Self-Extrapolating Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05295)
*Yang Li,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RISE方法，通过从模型自身RLVR训练轨迹中构造合成教师（在参数空间或输出logit空间中外推当前检查点与锚点之间的位移），将稀疏的基于结果的参数更新转化为稠密的token级目标，将RLVR与OPD结合形成互补循环，实现递归式改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略蒸馏（OPD）提供稠密的token级监督，但其效果受限于教师质量：外部教师存在分布不匹配问题，而带特权条件的自蒸馏受限于上下文学习能力。需要一种不依赖外部模型或特权条件的高质量稠密监督方法。

Method: RISE从模型自身RLVR训练轨迹构造合成教师，通过外推当前检查点与拖后锚点之间的位移（在参数空间或logit空间），将稀疏的结果级奖励转化为稠密的token级蒸馏目标。RISE将RLVR与OPD构成互补循环：结果奖励将外推锚定到正确推理方向，外推教师则细化token级决策。教师每轮迭代更新，使蒸馏成为递归改进机制。

Result: 在数学推理、多领域STEM、代码生成和多轮智能体任务上，RISE均优于仅RLVR训练和on-policy自蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: RISE通过从训练轨迹中递归外推构造合成教师，有效解决了外部教师分布不匹配和自蒸馏能力受限的问题，建立了结果监督与token级蒸馏互补的改进循环机制。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense, per-token supervision for language model post-training, but its effectiveness is bottlenecked by teacher quality: external teachers suffer from distribution mismatch, while self-distillation with privileged conditioning is limited by in-context learning capacity. We propose \textbf{RISE} (\textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{I}mprovement via \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{E}xtrapolating Policy Distillation), which constructs a synthetic teacher directly from the model's own RLVR training trajectory. By extrapolating the displacement between the current checkpoint and a trailing anchor---in parameter space or output logit space---RISE converts a sparse outcome-induced parameter update into a dense token-level target, without any external model or privileged conditioning. RISE combines RLVR and OPD in a complementary loop: outcome rewards ground the extrapolation toward correct reasoning, while the extrapolated teacher refines token-level decisions. Moreover, since the teacher is refreshed every iteration as the student improves, distillation becomes a recursive improvement mechanism rather than a one-shot compression step. Experiments spanning mathematical reasoning, multi-domain STEM, code generation, and multi-turn agentic tasks show that RISE outperforms RLVR-only training and on-policy self-distillation across all settings.

</details>


### [324] [LLM-Driven Algorithm Design for Quantum Circuit Synthesis based on Binary Decision Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05327)
*Yoonju Sim,Federico Berto,Chuanbo Hua,Jinkyoo Park,Changhyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuantumEvo框架，利用LLM作为启发式生成器，为量子电路合成中的BDD变量排序搜索QCC感知的启发式方法，发现的新启发式HGA-QE通过改进遗传算法中的sifting步骤，在基准测试中表现出优越的量子电路成本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BDD变量排序启发式主要优化BDD大小，但BDD大小只是合成电路量子成本（QCC）的不完美代理，无法准确反映实际量子电路的开销，因此需要直接针对QCC进行优化的变量排序方法。

Method: 提出QuantumEvo进化框架：使用LLM作为启发式生成器，从多个启发式族初始化搜索空间；候选启发式通过标准BDD操作直接操纵变量排序，并以下游QCC作为选择标准进行进化搜索。

Result: 发现的启发式HGA-QE在基准测试中相对于每个函数的最佳基线达到70.9%的平局或胜出率，在13.5%的函数上严格最优；在两个来自启发式发现数据之外来源的基准套件上表现出更明显的严格胜出优势。

Conclusion: 结果表明直接用QCC指导启发式搜索比用BDD大小代理更有效，LLM驱动的进化搜索可以发现与量子成本更对齐的变量排序启发式，且发现的启发式具备跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Quantum circuits are central to implementing quantum algorithms on quantum devices, where quantum gates must be reversible. Many quantum algorithms rely on Boolean functions, which must therefore be implemented reversibly within quantum circuits. Reversible circuit synthesis provides a way to translate such Boolean functions into reversible circuits. Binary decision diagrams (BDDs) offer a scalable approach to this task, but the resulting BDDs and circuits depend heavily on variable ordering. Existing ordering heuristics commonly minimize BDD size because it is closely tied to the circuit size. However, BDD size is an imperfect proxy for the quantum cost of the synthesized circuit (QCC). We propose \texttt{QuantumEvo}, an evolutionary framework that uses an LLM as a heuristic generator for QCC-aware BDD variable ordering. Instead of predicting orderings directly, \texttt{QuantumEvo} searches over ordering heuristics initialized from multiple heuristic families. Candidate heuristics directly manipulate variable orderings using standard BDD operations and are selected by downstream QCC. The discovered heuristic, HGA-QE, modifies the sifting step inside a genetic algorithm so that the procedure is better aligned with QCC. Across the benchmark set, HGA-QE achieves a 70.9\% tie-or-win rate against the per-function best baseline and is strictly best on 13.5\% of the functions. The results demonstrate broadly competitive QCC performance, with HGA-QE showing a clearer relative advantage in strict wins on the two benchmark suites drawn from sources different from the data used for heuristic discovery.

</details>


### [325] [Who Should Grade My Work? Student Perspectives on Transparent AI-Assisted Writing Assessment in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05346)
*Rayed AlGhamdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了当学生明确知晓评分和反馈由AI（ChatGPT）而非人类教师生成时，他们如何理解、诠释和回应这种AI介导的评估。通过对沙特一所公立大学13名男性计算机本科生的质性研究，发现学生认可AI反馈对表层修改有用，但始终认为人类教师才是评分决策的恰当权威，形成了"反馈效用"与"评价权威"的分离判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已考察GenAI反馈能否提升写作表现及学生对其有用性的感知，但鲜有研究关注当学生明确被告知评分与反馈来自AI系统而非人类教师时，他们如何解读这种评估。这一认知空白正是本研究的出发点。

Method: 在沙特一所公立大学本科技术传播课程中开展质性教学探究。13名男性计算机专业本科生完成课堂手写写作任务，扫描后由ChatGPT依据与任务目标对齐的rubric提示进行评分并提供反馈。随后明确告知学生评分与反馈由ChatGPT生成，并邀请他们书面反思此次评估。采用归纳主题分析处理反思文本。

Result: 识别出四个主题：反馈感知有用性、对AI情境与教学局限性的认知、条件性信任（区分反馈效用与评价权威）、对人类教师制度与教学角色的反思。学生接受AI反馈有助于表层修订，但一致认为人类教师应掌握评分决策的权威。

Conclusion: 研究提出"反馈效用"与"评价权威"是学生对待AI评估时在分析上截然分离的两种判断，而非同一认可轴上的对立两端。这一区分对高等教育中AI评估工具的整合设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: The integration of GenAI tools into higher education assessment raises important questions about how students understand, interpret, and respond to AI-mediated evaluation. As instructors increasingly explore AI tools for providing feedback, prior research has examined whether GenAI-generated feedback improves writing performance and how students perceive its usefulness; comparatively little is known, however, about how students interpret such evaluation when they are explicitly informed that an AI system, rather than a human instructor, produced the feedback and the score. This study reports findings from a qualitative pedagogical inquiry conducted in an undergraduate technical communication course for computing students at a Saudi public university. Thirteen male undergraduate computing students completed an in-class handwritten writing task; the scanned submissions were evaluated by ChatGPT using a rubric-based prompt aligned with the task objectives. Students were then explicitly informed that ChatGPT had generated the score and feedback and were invited to reflect on the evaluation in writing. Inductive thematic analysis of these reflections identified four themes: perceived usefulness of feedback; awareness of AI's contextual and pedagogical limitations; conditional trust, distinguishing feedback utility from evaluative authority; and reflection on the institutional and pedagogical role of the human instructor. Participants accepted GenAI feedback as useful for surface-level revision but consistently positioned the human instructor as the appropriate authority over grading decisions. The study identifies this as a distinction between feedback utility and evaluative authority, two judgments that students treat as analytically separate rather than as opposite ends of a single approval scale...

</details>


### [326] [CUA-Universe: A Scalable and Dynamic Environment for Hybrid GUI+CLI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05374)
*Haoting Shi,Wenhao Wang,Weicheng Fang,Yaozhong Liang,Tian Jin,Pengxiang Zhao,Guangyi Liu,Siheng Chen,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CUA-Universe，一个可扩展的环境到数据管道，将真实桌面软件转化为混合GUI+CLI环境，通过App-Forge、Task-Weave和Path-Steer三个组件构建训练数据，使9B模型在GUI与CLI之间高效协同操作，在多个基准上显著提升成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体大多只依赖GUI操作，轨迹低效；而真实世界的计算机工作本质上是混合的，需要协调视觉状态检查与高吞吐的命令行操作。然而，同时支持GUI和CLI的可扩展混合环境稀缺，因为每个应用都需要大量人工工程。此外，CLI原生智能体缺乏视觉感知，GUI原生智能体在命令可更好完成的操作上效率低下。

Method: 提出CUA-Universe管道，包含三个核心组件：(1) App-Forge，将应用适配为可复现的VM并发现、包装或生成命令行界面，扩展到16个应用；(2) Task-Weave，从种子文件的可重用操作中合成难度可控的多样化混合任务；(3) Path-Steer，引导rollout沿高效混合路径执行并收集验证轨迹用于后训练。

Result: 9B模型在CUA-Verse上Score提升39.3分，步骤减少37%，token减少60%；在OSWorld上SR提升16.8分，步骤减少57%，token减少44%；在OSWorld-MCP上Score提升7.84分，步骤减少27%，token减少30%。

Conclusion: CUA-Universe提供了迈向更强、更高效计算机使用智能体的可扩展路径，训练使行为从低效的GUI交互和脆弱的CLI脚本转向有效的GUI+CLI协同编排。

Abstract: Computer-use agents have advanced on benchmarks like OSWorld and AndroidWorld, but still act mostly through the GUI, often producing inefficient trajectories. Real-world computer work is hybrid, combining visual-state inspection with precise, high-throughput command-line operations, so capable agents must coordinate both modalities over shared application state. Yet scalable hybrid environments remain scarce because supporting both GUI and CLI over real applications typically requires substantial manual engineering for each application. Existing agents also struggle to use the two interfaces complementarily: CLI-native agents lack visual perception for tasks involving interface state or layout, while GUI-native agents are inefficient for operations better executed through commands. We introduce CUA-Universe, a scalable environment-to-data pipeline that turns real desktop software into hybrid GUI+CLI environments. App-Forge adapts applications into reproducible VMs and command-line surfaces it discovers, wraps, or generates, scaling to 16 applications; Task-Weave synthesizes diverse hybrid tasks of controllable difficulty from reusable operations over seed files; and Path-Steer steers rollouts along efficient hybrid paths and harvests verified trajectories for post-training. Training on this data shifts behavior from inefficient GUI interaction and brittle CLI scripting toward effective GUI+CLI orchestration. Our 9B model improves both success and efficiency on CUA-Verse (Score +39.3 pts; -37% steps, -60% tokens), OSWorld (SR +16.8 pts; -57% steps, -44% tokens), and OSWorld-MCP (Score +7.84 pts; -27% steps, -30% tokens). CUA-Universe provides a scalable path toward more capable and efficient computer-use agents.

</details>


### [327] [Molecular Déjà Vu: Digit-Level Retrieval of Published Values in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05381)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Christian J. Cyron,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了22个前沿大语言模型在12个分子回归基准上的逐字检索（verbatim retrieval）现象，发现该问题普遍存在但具有基准特异性。推理水平会影响检索行为（高推理水平下检索被标记的频率比低水平高89%）。此外，抑制检索会使模型间的预测误差相对接近，表明模型预测能力并非仅由记忆值决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在分子属性基准上的高准确率无法区分模型是真正预测了属性还是检索了已发表的数据。这引发了关于基准评估可信度的担忧，需要系统审计LLM是否存在逐字检索已发表数值的行为。

Method: 对22个前沿模型在12个回归基准上进行逐字检索审计；在两个不同推理水平下重复实验以观察推理对检索的影响；在最严重污染案例中尝试打断检索（如转换SMILES字符串）来测试模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 逐字检索普遍但具有基准特异性：5个数据集中超过50%的LLM显示检索行为，其余数据集仅在孤立单元中出现。高推理水平下检索被标记的频率比最低水平高89%。即使转换SMILES字符串，最强模型仍能识别原始标签的组合。抑制检索后，不同模型的预测误差在相对意义上更接近，而检索使用差异使其分散。

Conclusion: LLM在分子回归基准中存在广泛的逐字检索现象，其程度受基准和推理水平影响。模型的预测能力并非仅由记忆的数值量决定，这为理解LLM在分子属性预测中的真实能力提供了系统性概述。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on molecular property benchmarks, but accuracy cannot distinguish a model that predicts a property from one that retrieves a published number. We audit 22 frontier models on 12 regression benchmarks for verbatim retrieval and find that it is widespread but relatively benchmark-specific: on five datasets more than $50\%$ of the LLMs show verbatim retrieval, while on the remaining datasets it appears only in isolated cells. We run our experiments at two reasoning levels and find that reasoning changes retrieval. The same experiments, on the same molecules and with the same prompt, are flagged $89\%$ more often at the higher reasoning level than at the lowest one. Finally, we test a way to interrupt retrieval in our most contaminated cases, and find that the strongest models in some cases still recognise a combination of transformed SMILES strings and original labels. Furthermore, suppressing retrieval moves the prediction errors of the different models closer together in relative terms, while their differing use of verbatim retrieval spreads them apart. This indicates that the general predictive capability of an LLM is not determined solely by the amount of memorised values. This work provides an overview of the amount and depth of verbatim retrieval in molecular regression benchmarks using LLMs.

</details>


### [328] [Necessary or Sufficient? Evaluating LLM Explanations With Behavioural Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05385)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Nabeel Kemal,Ashwin Kandath,Owen O'Neill,Guillaume Bourgeon,Houssem Chatbri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在agent工作流中生成的解释性因素是否与实际决策行为一致。通过必要性（改变因素会改变输出）和充分性（保留因素而移除其他信息仍保持输出）两种解释，在两个合成用例（顾问推荐和提示词风险判断）中对Claude、GPT和Gemini家族的8个模型进行黑盒干预测试。结果显示引用的因素排名与实际测量影响之间的Spearman相关性仅为0.349-0.580，未引用因素在57.6%的顾问响应中得分高于最低的引用因素，表明解释提供有用信息但不能可靠识别最具影响力的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在agent工作流中，LLM生成的决策解释常被操作员用于监控系统、诊断错误或决定何时升级输出。这种使用隐含假设了解释与实际决策行为一致，但这一假设从未被严格验证。作者旨在建立一种可测试的框架来检验LLM解释的可靠性，特别是在agent监督场景下。

Method: 提出两种解释语义：必要性（改变因素会改变输出）和充分性（保留该因素并移除其他可变信息仍保持输出）。在两个合成用例中，让模型返回输出及影响最大的前三个因素，然后通过受控黑盒干预分别计算每个因素的必要性分数（改变它时输出改变频率）和充分性分数（保留它时输出保持频率），并与模型引用的排名进行Spearman相关分析。

Result: 跨8个模型，引用的因素排名与必要性和充分性分数的平均Spearman相关性在顾问推荐用例中为0.349和0.354，在提示词监控用例中为0.431和0.580。未引用因素在57.6%（顾问，必要性下）和58.1%（顾问，充分性下）的响应中高于最低引用的因素；提示词监控的对应比例分别为25.8%和8.9%。引用的前三个因素包含有用信息，但不能可靠识别测量影响最强的三个因素。

Conclusion: LLM生成的解释性因素在agent监督场景中提供了一定信息但缺乏可靠性保证。该框架可以作为解释的黑盒可靠性检验，适用于agent监督，但范围限制在单个LLM决策层面，不能推广到更广泛的agent行为验证。

Abstract: LLM decision components that can operate within agent workflows often produce action-relevant recommendations or judgements together with explanations. Operators may use the named factors to monitor a system, diagnose errors, or decide when to escalate an output. Such use assumes that the explanations agree with the component's observable decision behaviour. We test two interpretations of the named factors: necessity, meaning that changing a factor would change the output, and sufficiency, meaning that retaining it while removing other changeable information would preserve the output. We evaluate these interpretations in two synthetic use cases: recommending advisors to clients and judging prompts for harmfulness or risk. Models return an output and the top three factors that most influenced it. Controlled black-box interventions estimate a necessity score for each factor by measuring how often changing it changes the output, and a sufficiency score by measuring how often retaining it preserves the output. Across eight models from the Claude, GPT, and Gemini families, the mean Spearman correlations between the cited ranking and the necessity and sufficiency scores are 0.349 and 0.354 for advisor recommendation, and 0.431 and 0.580 for prompt monitoring. Furthermore, an uncited factor scores above the lowest-scoring cited factor in 57.6% of advisor responses under necessity and 58.1% under sufficiency; the corresponding prompt-monitoring rates are 25.8% and 8.9%. The cited top three contain useful information but do not reliably identify the three factors with the strongest measured influence under necessity or sufficiency. The framework provides a black-box reliability check for explanations used in agent oversight while remaining scoped to individual LLM decisions.

</details>


### [329] [A Deep Generative Model for Synthesizing Labeled Wireless Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05396)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的无线信号生成方法 IIns-GAN（Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks），用于生成具有真实物理特性的带标签无线信号，以解决真实数据集采集和标注成本高昂、传统合成方法依赖环境模型且真实感不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线感知领域需要带位置标签的无线信号用于性能评估和模型训练，但真实数据集的采集和标注成本高昂；传统基于环境模型的合成方法需要大量超参数调优且生成的信号真实感不足，难以支撑全面的模型训练任务。

Method: 提出 IIns-GAN，一种基于深度学习（生成对抗网络）的跨实例信号生成方法，能够生成适应不同环境场景的带标签真实无线信号。

Result: 在公开的 UWB（超宽带）数据集上进行的大量实验表明，IIns-GAN 生成的信号能够反映真实测量的物理特性，并显著提升距离估计和环境识别等无线感知任务的模型训练效果。

Conclusion: IIns-GAN 有效缓解了无线感知领域真实数据获取困难的瓶颈，生成的高质量带标签信号可用于多种模型训练任务，具有较好的环境适应性和实用价值。

Abstract: Wireless signals with position-related labels are pivotal for both performance evaluation and model training in the realm of wireless sensing. However, acquiring real-world datasets is often challenged by significant measurement and labeling costs. Traditional methods for synthesizing labeled wireless signals typically rely on environmental models, leading to extensive hyper-parameter tuning and inadequate realism for comprehensive model training purposes. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel deep learning (DL)-based method, namely Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks (IIns-GAN), to generate realistic labeled wireless signals. The generated signals are particularly adaptive to different environment scenarios and well-suited for various model training tasks, including distance estimation and environment identification. We have conducted extensive experiments on public Ultra-Wideband (UWB) datasets to evaluate the realism and utility of the generated signals. The results demonstrate that the signals generated by IIns-GAN mirror the physical characteristics of real-world measurements, and significantly contribute to the improvement of model training in diverse wireless sensing tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [330] [DTM: Deterministic Approaches for Black-box Test Suite Minimization with Tree-based Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04205)
*Md Siam,Shartaz Sajid Nahid,Md Arif Hasan,Nurul Ahad Tawhid,Kazi Sakib*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DTM框架，通过将测试用例转换为抽象语法树并计算四种树形相似度，结合三种确定性算法（改进最小生成树、谱聚类、动态规划）实现黑盒测试套件最小化，在16个Java项目的661个缺陷版本上达到0.74的平均准确率，执行时间仅0.98分钟，同时保证完全确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒测试套件最小化（TSM）方法虽然有效，但依赖进化搜索算法，引入非确定性，导致多次运行结果不一致，削弱了自动化测试管道的可靠性。

Method: 将测试用例转化为抽象语法树，使用四种基于树的度量计算两两相似度；采用三种确定性算法（改进最小生成树、谱聚类、动态规划）进行子集选择。

Result: 在16个Java项目（来自Defects4J，661个缺陷版本）上评估，平均准确率达0.74，执行时间仅0.98分钟，优于所有现有最优方法，且多次运行结果完全一致。

Conclusion: DTM在保持测试套件最小化的有效性和效率的同时，解决了传统进化算法方法的不确定性问题，为自动化测试管道提供了可靠且确定性的解决方案。

Abstract: Black-box Test Suite Minimization (TSM) techniques reduce testing costs without requiring access to production code. However, existing effective approaches rely on evolutionary search algorithms, introducing non-determinism that produces inconsistent results across runs, undermining reliability in automated testing pipelines. We propose DTM (Deterministic approaches for black-box Test suite Minimization), a framework that ensures deterministic test suite reduction while preserving effectiveness and efficiency. DTM converts test cases into Abstract Syntax Trees and computes pairwise similarities using four tree-based measures. For subset selection, it employs three deterministic algorithms: Modified Minimum Spanning Tree, Spectral Clustering, and Dynamic Programming. We evaluated DTM on 16 Java projects from Defects4J with 661 buggy versions. Experimental results show that DTM achieved an average accuracy of 0.74 with an execution time of just 0.98 minutes, outperforming all state-of-the-art approaches. Moreover, it consistently produced identical results across multiple runs, ensuring full determinism.

</details>


### [331] [Breaking the Alphabet: Rethinking File Ordering in Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04207)
*Md Shamimur Rahman,Zadia Codabux,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模调查（1,355名专业开发者，182个开源项目）首次系统研究了Pull Request中文件排序方式对代码审查有效性的影响。研究发现仅10.2%的审查者认为字母排序是最优方案，57.6%认为它会增加上下文切换和审查疲劳，63.9%担心会导致漏掉bug，66%的审查者期望依赖感知分组和可自定义的文件排序功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码审查对软件质量至关重要，但关于PR中变更文件排序如何影响审查有效性的研究非常有限。主流工具默认使用字母排序，这种排序偏向可预测性而非上下文关联性，其对人认知负荷和审查全面性感知的影响尚不明确。

Method: 采用混合方法（mixed-methods），对182个广泛使用的开源项目中的1,355名专业开发者进行大规模调查，分析文件排序对审查行为、理解和感知有效性的影响。

Result: （1）仅10.2%审查者认为字母排序最优；（2）57.6%报告字母排序增加上下文切换、扰乱逻辑推理并导致审查疲劳；（3）63.9%担心默认排序可能导致漏掉bug；（4）66%审查者期望依赖感知分组和可自定义排序功能。

Conclusion: 当前默认的字母排序与人类认知存在错配，需要审查者中心的界面设计，使工具行为更好地符合人类认知模式，包括依赖感知分组和可自定义的文件排序。

Abstract: Effective code review is central to maintaining software quality, yet there is limited research about how the ordering of changed files in Pull Requests (PRs) influences review effectiveness. Most popular code review tools default to alphabetical ordering, favoring predictability over contextual relevance. While prior studies examined how file position shapes reviewer attention, it remains unclear how such ordering influences cognitive load and perceived review thoroughness. This study presents the first large-scale survey of 1,355 professional developers across 182 widely used open-source projects to investigate how file ordering impacts review behavior, comprehension, and perceived effectiveness. Our mixed-methods analysis reveals that only 10.2% of reviewers consider alphabetical ordering optimal, underscoring a cognitive misalignment in their interpretation of code changes. Although some developers appreciate its predictability, more than half (57.6%) report that it increases context switching, disrupts logical reasoning, and contributes to review fatigue, and 63.9% expressed concern that the default ordering may cause them to miss bugs. We further identify key challenges in multi-file reviews and elicit developers' expectations for improved tooling, including dependency-aware grouping and customizable file ordering (requested by 66% of reviewers). These findings highlight the need for reviewer-centric interface designs that better align tool behavior with human cognition.

</details>


### [332] [AI Writes Code, Humans Pay the Debt. An Empirical Study on the Sustainability and Evolution of Agent-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04208)
*Antonino Coppola,Matteo Esposito,Rick Kazman,Valentina Lenarduzzi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出一项大规模软件仓库挖掘研究，利用SQuaD数据集和628k个issue工单，通过系统性基准测试选择多个LLM智能体生成代码实现，并与开发者实际提交进行对比，以评估智能体生成代码对软件质量问题定位准确性、技术债务引入及其长期演化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI编码智能体在软件工程中的采用日益增长，带来短期生产力提升，但其对软件质量和长期技术债务的影响尚不明确，缺乏系统的实证证据。

Method: 在大规模软件仓库上进行挖掘研究，采用SQuaD数据集和628k个issue工单候选集，使用多个LLM智能体生成代码实现，与开发者的实际提交在commit级别和release级别进行对比，采用静态分析指标和工具进行分析，智能体通过系统性基准测试策略选择。

Result: 预期提供智能体驱动开发所带来的权衡的实证证据，包括问题定位精度的差异、技术债务引入的变化以及长期演化的潜在分歧；预期结果将揭示不同LLM之间的变异性。

Conclusion: 研究成果将丰富对智能体参与软件演化的理解，并为软件工程中更负责任地采用智能体提供指导信息。

Abstract: Context. The increasing adoption of Generative AI coding agents in software engineering is transforming how developers implement and maintain code. While these systems provide short-term productivity benefits, their long-term impact on software quality and technical debt remains unclear. Aim. We aim to investigate how agent-generated code affects software quality, focusing on issue localization accuracy, the introduction of technical debt, and its evolution over time. Method. We will conduct a large-scale mining software repositories study using the SQuaD dataset, employing a candidate set of 628k issue tickets. We will generate agent-based implementations for these issues, and compare them with the actual commits done by developers using static analysis metrics and tools. We will analyze differences at the commit level and across releases, and we will consider multiple LLM-based Agents selected through a systematic benchmarking strategy. Expected Results. We expect to provide empirical evidence on the trade-offs introduced by agent-based development, including differences in localization accuracy, variations in technical debt introduction, and potential divergence in long-term evolution. We expect the results to highlight variability across LLMs, to enrich our understanding of software evolution with Agents, and to inform more responsible adoption of Agents in software development.

</details>


### [333] [The Prompt Triangle: A Registered Report on Prompts as Hybrid Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04209)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Jan-Philipp Steghöfer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项注册报告（Stage 1协议），提出了一项验证性研究，探讨基于AI的编程辅助中提示词（prompts）如何作为软件工程工件演化，以及其与需求工程（RE）活动的一致性如何预测开发成果。研究基于"Prompt Triangle"概念模型，预设四个假设，采用受控实验、社区上传数据和挖掘数据的双编码方法进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI编程助手日益普及，将开发者的工作从编写代码转向编写提示词，但提示词作为软件工程工件如何运作缺乏实证证据。作者希望填补这一空白，理解提示词如何演化，以及其与需求工程对齐程度对开发成果的影响。

Method: 本研究为一篇注册报告（Registered Report）的Stage 1协议。基于Prompt Triangle概念模型（功能与质量、通用解决方案、具体解决方案三个维度），预先注册四个假设（提示词演化、开发者特征、RE活动对齐、时间模式），采用三重复合数据源：受控实验（n=30）、社区上传数据以及挖掘数据，使用双编码方法检验提示模式是否能预测开发成功。

Result: 由于本文是Stage 1注册报告协议，尚无实证结果。研究目的是检验提示模式与需求工程活动对齐程度能否预测开发成功。

Conclusion: 本文推进了将提示词视为混合软件工程工件（融合需求意图与解决方案引导）的研究方向，通过预先注册的验证性研究方法论，为理解AI辅助开发中提示词工程与需求工程的关联提供了系统化的实证研究框架。

Abstract: AI-based coding assistants are transforming software development by shifting effort from writing code to crafting prompts that guide code generation. Despite their growing importance, little empirical evidence exists on how prompts function as software engineering artifacts. Building on prior work framing prompts as mixed artifacts combining requirement intent and solution guidance, we build on the Prompt Triangle, a conceptual model describing prompts along three components: Functionality and Quality (requirements), General Solutions (architectural guidance), and Specific Solutions (implementation constraints). This registered report presents the Stage 1 protocol for a confirmatory study investigating how prompts evolve and how their alignment with requirements engineering (RE) activities predicts development outcomes. We preregister four hypotheses examining prompt evolution, developer characteristics, RE activity alignment, and temporal patterns. We employ a controlled experiment (n=30), community uploads, and mined data using dual-coding to test whether prompting patterns predict development success.

</details>


### [334] [SH-PDOPS: AI-Driven Cloud Native Enterprise Reliability Framework for Predictive Analytics and Intelligent DevOps Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04210)
*Ayushman Bosu Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个AI驱动的云原生企业可靠性框架，集成机器学习、Kubernetes编排、可观测性管道和自动化事件响应机制，以提升预测分析、智能DevOps自动化和系统弹性。实验证明该框架能提高运营效率、减少停机时间并增强企业环境可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式基础设施日益复杂，传统可靠性工程方法难以应对大规模动态环境中的故障预测、异常检测和自动化运维需求。作者旨在通过融合AI与DevOps方法论，实现自适应和自主的基础设施管理，解决企业级系统在可靠性运维方面的挑战。

Method: 该框架将机器学习模型与Kubernetes容器编排相结合，构建完整的可观测性管道，并嵌入自动化事件响应机制。具体涵盖预测性故障检测、异常监控、自愈基础设施以及CI/CD流程优化等核心模块，以云原生技术为底层支撑。

Result: 实验评估表明，该框架能够显著提升企业环境的运营效率、减少系统停机时间，并增强整体可扩展性，验证了AI与DevOps结合在实际企业基础设施管理中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为将人工智能融入可靠性工程实践提供了实用路径，展现了云原生技术栈下实现自适应、自主基础设施管理的可行性与应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven cloud-native enterprise reliability framework designed to improve predictive analytics, intelligent DevOps automation, and system resilience in modern distributed infrastructures. The proposed framework integrates machine learning models, Kubernetes orchestration, observability pipelines, and automated incident response mechanisms to enhance reliability engineering practices. The study explores predictive failure detection, anomaly monitoring, self-healing infrastructure, and CI/CD optimization using cloud-native technologies. Experimental evaluation demonstrates improved operational efficiency, reduced downtime, and enhanced scalability for enterprise environments. The framework provides a practical approach for combining artificial intelligence with DevOps methodologies to achieve adaptive and autonomous infrastructure management.

</details>


### [335] [Data-Related Challenges and Requirements for Event Log Generation in Process Mining: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04211)
*Ghita El Alaoui Talibi,Oleksandr Kosenkov,Anastasija Nikiforova*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对流程挖掘中事件日志生成阶段的数据相关挑战、需求和解决方案进行了系统性文献综述，识别出29项挑战和5大类需求，指出现有解决方案已蕴含数据工程与领域知识工程技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程挖掘已成为支持业务流程改进和数字经济发展的关键技术，而数据质量是其最紧迫的挑战。然而事件日志生成作为流程挖掘的第一阶段，其与数据工程和需求工程的关系长期被忽视，需要系统性的梳理来填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，围绕事件日志生成中的数据相关挑战、需求和解决方案进行全面检索、筛选和分析，并通过分类归纳提炼挑战类型与需求类别。

Result: 识别出29项事件日志生成中的数据相关挑战，提出了5种基本类别的需求，并发现现有解决方案已内置多种数据工程和领域知识工程技术。

Conclusion: 该综述有助于研究人员和实践者理解事件日志生成在流程挖掘中的当前趋势，并指导其利用数据工程与需求工程技术改善日志生成效果，最终提升流程挖掘的整体质量。

Abstract: Process mining is becoming an essential technology that not only supports the improvement of business processes but also enables the software technologies underlying the future digital economy. Among the multiple challenges discussed by the process mining community, data quality is emerging as the most pressing one. However, insufficient attention has been paid to event log generation as the very first phase of process mining and to its relationship with data engineering and requirements engineering. To address this gap, we conducted a systematic literature review of data-related challenges, requirements, and solutions in event log generation. Our results show that a wide range of 29 challenges can be addressed with 5 basic categories of requirements. Existing solutions already embed some form of data engineering and domain knowledge engineering techniques for event log generation. This review can support researchers and practitioners in understanding current trends in event log generation for process mining and in leveraging data and requirements engineering techniques to improve log generation and, ultimately, process mining outcomes.

</details>


### [336] [Big Questions on Software Architecture: Report of the ICSE 2026 BoF on Software Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04212)
*Davide Taibi,Patricia Lago,Henry Muccini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个社区倡议的结果，旨在识别和讨论软件架构（SA）研究中的重大问题。该倡议于2026年在ICSE和ICSA两个会议上分两场举办，本文汇报第一场会议的成果，并期待与第二场会议的结果互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构的重要性日益突出，涉及技术对社会的影响、人机平衡、灵活性与混乱、经济便利与数字自主等广泛议题。然而，SA研究社区需要反思哪些是值得科学研究的大问题，哪些只是技术趋势或炒作。

Method: 通过社区倡议方式，在ICSE和ICSA两个会议（2026年）上组织两场讨论会话，收集并讨论SA研究中的重大问题。本文报告第一场会话的结果。

Result: 本文呈现了第一场会话（ICSE 2026）中收集到的SA研究重大问题的讨论结果，第二场会话（ICSA 2026）的结果尚未完成，后续将补充。

Conclusion: SA研究社区需要持续反思重大研究问题，区分真正的科学问题与技术趋势/炒作；本文是这一反思过程的第一步，更多结果有待第二场会议后补充。

Abstract: Software Architecture (SA) is more important than ever, from the need to master the pervasive impact of technology in society and planet earth, to balancing the role of humans versus technology, flexibility versus chaos, economic convenience versus digital autonomy. Despite SA importance, we feel that the SA research community needs to reflect on what are the big questions that deserve scientific research, and what are instead technology trends or hypes. In this paper, we report the results of a community initiative aiming to identify and discuss the big questions in SA research. Organised in two sessions hosted in 2026 at the ICSE and ICSA conferences, respectively, this paper reports on the results collected during the first session, and looks forward to complementing them with the results of the second one. To be continued ...

</details>


### [337] [Toward Model-Driven Digital Twin Configuration: Separating Structure Semantics and Runtime with SysML SAREF and Ditto](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04213)
*Andrey Sadovykh,Matthew Rusakov,Kirill Korikov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将能源社区数字孪生配置任务拆分为三个互补工具：SysML建模语言负责结构、SAREF4ENER标准词汇负责语义、Eclipse Ditto（基于W3C Web-of-Things Thing Description）负责运行表示，以取代手工编写配置的传统做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数字孪生的搭建高度依赖人工：工程师手写配置，字段名随意、单位隐含、缺乏机器可读的设备关系描述，导致其他系统无法可靠解析，难以实现互操作性和可追溯性。

Method: 提出一套概念性规则（尚未实现为可执行生成器），从标注了SAREF4ENER词汇的SysML模型中推导出Ditto配置；并用Modelio建模的温室能源社区节点作为示例进行说明。

Result: 论证了这种拆分方法相比手工编写方案，能够提升可追溯性和配置一致性，并为互操作性建立语义钩子；贡献范围限定在设设计时（design-time）配置，未覆盖运行时。

Conclusion: 该位置论文主张"各工具做各自最擅长的事"的职责分离思路，即用SysML描述结构、SAREF4ENER定义语义、Ditto承载运行表示，能有效改善数字孪生配置的质量，但当前仅为概念框架，尚待实现与验证。

Abstract: Energy communities, neighbourhoods that jointly produce, store, and consume electricity, increasingly rely on \emph{digital twins}: live software copies of physical devices such as a solar panel, a battery, or a heater, kept in sync for monitoring and control. Setting up such a twin is today mostly manual: an engineer hand-writes a configuration whose field names are arbitrary, whose units are implicit, and which carries no machine-readable description of how devices relate, so other systems cannot reliably interpret it. This position paper argues that the task should be split across three complementary tools, each doing what it does best: a modelling language for the system's structure (SysML), a standard energy vocabulary for its meaning (SAREF4ENER), and a digital-twin platform for the running representation (Eclipse Ditto, via the W3C Web-of-Things Thing Description). We give conceptual rules (not yet an executable generator) for deriving a Ditto configuration from a SysML model annotated with that vocabulary, illustrate them on a greenhouse energy-community node modelled in Modelio, and argue that the split improves traceability and configuration consistency, and establishes the semantic hooks needed for interoperability, over hand-written alternatives. We scope the contribution to design-time configuration and discuss its limits.

</details>


### [338] [A Mixed-Method Empirical Study of LLM Assistance in Software Engineering Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04214)
*Pamali D. Weerasinghe,Roshan N. Rajapakse,Isuru Dharmadasa,Chamath Keppitiyagama*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法实证研究，考察大语言模型（LLM）辅助软件工程对不同年级本科生（大一与大四）的影响。研究包括第一阶段问卷调查（n=157）和第二阶段基于任务的准实验（n=20），对比AI辅助与非AI条件下在实现、约束驱动算法选择及架构推理三类任务中的表现与行为轨迹，发现LLM辅助的收益具有任务依赖性，并受开发者专业水平与验证实践的中介影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛融入软件开发流程，但现有讨论往往未区分任务类型、开发者资历和验证需求，导致对其影响的理解过于笼统。作者旨在通过区分这些维度，提供更细致的实证洞察。

Method: 采用混合方法设计：第一阶段为初步问卷调查（n=157），刻画两组学生对LLM的接触、依赖与信任校准差异；第二阶段为任务型准实验（n=20），对两类群体（大一与大四）在AI辅助与非AI条件下执行涵盖实现、约束驱动算法选择和架构推理的结构化任务集，结合屏幕录制的行为追踪与定性编码分析。

Result: 调查显示LLM广泛采用且伴随大量验证工作，同时存在群体间对约束密集型场景下LLM能力的感知差异。准实验表明AI辅助改变工作流结构：参与者常采用AI优先任务入口、复制-传递集成和AI中介调试，而非AI工作流更依赖文档、既有模板和迭代试错。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的收益取决于任务类型，并受专业水平与验证实践所中介，而非仅由生成速度决定。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software development workflows, yet their effects are often discussed without distinguishing between task types, developer seniority, and verification demands. This paper presents a mixed-method empirical study of LLM-assisted software engineering with first-year and fourth-year undergraduates. Phase 1 is a preliminary survey (n=157) that characterizes LLM exposure, reliance, and trust calibration among the two groups. Phase 2 is a task-based quasi-experiment with a purposive sample from both cohorts (n=20). Here, we compare AI-assisted and non-AI conditions on a structured set of software engineering tasks spanning implementation, constraint-driven algorithm selection, and architectural reasoning. We then analyze performance outcomes alongside behavioral traces captured via screen recording and a qualitative coding process. Survey results indicate widespread LLM adoption and substantial verification effort, alongside cohort differences in perceived LLM capability for constraint-heavy scenarios. The quasi-experiment further shows that AI assistance changes workflow structure. For example, participants frequently adopt AI-first task entry, copy-transfer integration, and AI-mediated debugging, whereas non-AI workflows rely more on documentation, prior templates, and iterative trial-error refinement. Overall, our findings suggest that the benefits of LLM assistance are task-dependent and mediated by expertise and verification practices, rather than by generation speed alone.

</details>


### [339] [A systematic literature review on logging smell detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04215)
*Nora Madi,Manal Binkhonain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述（SLR）对21项关于日志异味检测的研究进行了全面梳理，定义了关键术语、将异味类型映射到现有分类体系，并分析了检测技术、数据集和评估策略。研究发现该领域研究分散且缺乏统一基准，最后指出挑战并提出未来方向，如开发更好的工具、利用LLM和建立标准化数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志记录是软件开发的重要组成部分，但糟糕的日志实现会产生"日志异味"（logging smells），降低日志可用性甚至引发问题。目前对这一领域的研究缺乏系统性的理解和综合，作者希望通过SLR全面了解现有的自动检测技术、数据集和评估方法，以填补这一知识空白。

Method: 采用系统文献综述（Systematic Literature Review, SLR）方法，对21项聚焦日志异味检测的研究进行深入分析。具体包括：定义关键日志相关术语、将识别出的异味类型映射到已有分类体系（taxonomy）、并审查各研究中使用的检测技术、数据集和评估策略。

Result: 研究发现该领域研究仍较为分散且不一致。主要问题包括：缺乏通用的基准（benchmark）或标准化的评估方法，导致不同研究难以相互比较；此外，各研究在目标异味类型和数量上存在不一致，处理方式缺乏统一性。

Conclusion: 日志异味的研究和检测仍有较大改进空间。作者指出了若干挑战，并建议未来研究方向包括：开发更完善的检测工具、探索大型语言模型（LLMs）在该领域的应用、以及构建更标准化的评估数据集以促进研究可比性。

Abstract: Context:Logging is an important part of software development that helps developers monitor systems, understand behavior, and fix problems. But when logging is done poorly, it can introduce logging smells, which are defects that reduce the usefulness of logs or even make them problematic. Objective:This study looks at how logging smells are currently detected. The goal is to better understand the existing research on automatic detection techniques, datasets, and evaluation methods. Method:We conducted a systematic literature review (SLR) of 21 studies focused on detecting logging smells. In this review, we define key logging-related terms, identify and map the types of smells to an existing taxonomy, and examine the detection techniques, datasets, and evaluation strategies used across the studies. Results:We found that the research is still scattered and inconsistent. For example, there is no common benchmark or standardized approach for evaluating results, making it difficult to compare studies.
