Method: (1)构建AVENUE基准：从VGGSound中人工筛选验证1,291个源片段和7,957条编辑指令，覆盖音频定向、视频定向、音视频耦合三种编辑类型；(2)设计样本特定、模态感知的评估框架，对每个样本明确指定预期变更内容与必须保持不变的模态内容；(3)对联合、顺序、分离三种主流生成范式的代表模型进行系统评估。

Result: 对三种编辑范式（联合、顺序、分离）的模型进行首次系统性模态选择性分析，发现一个根本性开放挑战：无论采用何种范式，现有模型在编辑单一模态时都频繁对另一模态产生非预期改动。

Conclusion: AVENUE提供了更全面的基准和模态感知评估框架，用于推动可控AV编辑模型的发展。数据集已在Hugging Face上公开。

Abstract: Audio-video (AV) editing aims to modify audio and video content according to a target prompt. Unlike single-modality editing, AV editing requires models to infer a modality-selective edit scope from the prompt alone: determining not only what should change, but also which modality should be preserved. Faithfully evaluating such models therefore requires both (i) benchmarks that span diverse edit types and modality categories, and (ii) evaluation that is itself modality-aware and sample-specific. However, existing AV editing benchmarks provide limited coverage of edit types and modality combinations, while current evaluation systems are often modality-blind and sample-agnostic, making it difficult to assess whether models faithfully preserve the unintended modality. To address these gaps, we introduce AVENUE, Audio-Video EditiNg Understanding and Evaluation, comprising two contributions: (1) a benchmark of 1,291 source clips and 7,957 editing instructions across audio-targeted, video-targeted, and AV-coupled edit types, curated and human-verified from VGGSound; and (2) a sample-specific, modality-aware evaluation framework that specifies, for each sample, both the intended change and the content that must remain intact. We evaluate representative AV editing models spanning three editing paradigms : joint, sequential, and separate, providing the first systematic analysis of modality-selectivity across paradigms. Our findings reveal a fundamental open challenge: when editing one modality, existing models frequently induce unintended changes in the other, regardless of paradigm. AVENUE provides a benchmark and modality-aware evaluation framework to drive progress toward more controllable AV editing models. Our dataset is publicly available on Hugging Face: https://huggingface.co/datasets/AVENUE-dataset/AVENUE.

</details>


### [372] [PRISM-Bench: An Audio-Centric Diagnostic Benchmark for Text-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04867)
*Yuchen Sun,Qian Yang,Jun Wang,Detai Xin,Guoqiao Yu,Guanglu Wan,Qi Jia*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM-Bench，这是首个以音频为中心的文生音视频(T2AV)生成诊断基准。它基于900个人工验证样本，沿音频类型（语音、音乐、声音）和声源可见性（屏上/屏外）两个正交轴分解音频评估，通过35个细粒度标准在四个感知维度上评估生成内容，并采用增强的MLLM-as-a-Judge协议进行盲测对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2AV生成评估低估了音频模态：要么将音频视为视频质量的辅助组件，要么脱离视听接地关系孤立评估音频，导致难以诊断当前系统在音频生成上的真正成败。

Method: 构建900个人工验证样本的严格筛选数据集；沿音频类型和声源可见性两个正交轴分解评估；在四个感知维度（视听一致性、音频质量、音频表现力、提示遵循）上应用35个细粒度标准；采用基于盲测、与真值参考并排比较的增强MLLM-as-a-Judge评估协议。

Result: 该评估协议与人类评分者达到超过70%的平均一致性；对近期T2AV系统的评估揭示了前沿模型与开源模型之间存在显著性能差距；当前生成范式过度拟合感知保真度，但在复杂的接地和控制任务上表现不佳，尤其是在音乐生成和同步屏上音频方面。

Conclusion: PRISM-Bench提供了首个系统化的音频中心诊断框架，能够精确定位T2AV系统在音频生成上的具体缺陷，表明需要更多关注音频接地与控制能力而非仅追求感知保真度。

Abstract: Text-to-audio-video (T2AV) generation has advanced rapidly, but its evaluation still underestimates the audio modality. Existing benchmarks either treat audio as an auxiliary component of video quality or assess it in isolation from audiovisual grounding, making it difficult to diagnose where current systems truly succeed or fail in audio generation. We present PRISM-Bench, the first audio-centric diagnostic benchmark for T2AV generation. Built from a rigorously curated dataset of 900 human-verified samples, PRISM-Bench factorizes audio evaluation along two orthogonal axes: audio type (Speech, Music, and Sound) and sound-source visibility (On-screen vs. Off-screen). It evaluates generated content across four perceptual dimensions (Audio-Visual Coherence, Audio Quality, Audio Expressiveness, and Prompt Following) with 35 fine-grained criteria. To ensure reliable assessment, we adopt an enhanced MLLM-as-a-Judge protocol based on blind, side-by-side comparison against ground-truth references, demonstrating strong alignment (over 70% mean agreement) with human raters. Our evaluation of recent T2AV systems highlights a significant performance gap between frontier and open-source models. Furthermore, we demonstrate that current generation paradigms overfit to perceptual fidelity while struggling with complex grounding and control tasks, particularly in generating music and synchronized On-screen audio.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [373] [SAGE: Semantic Attribute Graphs for Multi-Entity Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04255)
*Yongjoo Kim,Mincheol Kwon,Seonga Choi,Minseung Lee,Kyeong-Jin Oh,Hyunyoung Lee,Yunsu Choi,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE框架，通过将密集文档图像中的语义实体解析为层次图节点并使用多向量嵌入，以及迭代的实体级子图匹配，来解决密集文档图像中细粒度检索的"语义稀释"问题。同时引入DEAR数据集（1,055个查询-图像对）用于多实体视觉比较查询评估，SAGE在Recall@3上达到0.849。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的视觉-语言检索器用单一向量编码裁剪区域，在密集文档图像中会混合不同实体的信号，导致细粒度检索失败——即"语义稀释"（Semantic Dilution）问题。作者定量证明该问题随实体密度增加而恶化。

Method: SAGE是一个无需训练的框架：(1) 将密集文档图像解析为语义实体；(2) 将实体表示为层次图节点，并使用多向量嵌入；(3) 通过迭代的实体级子图匹配来检索查询相关的证据。还引入了DEAR数据集，包含来自产品详情页的1,055个查询-图像对，涵盖四种递增复杂度的问询类型。

Result: SAGE在DEAR上显著减少语义稀释，优于基于patch级别和基于OCR的检索基线；在多实体视觉比较查询上达到Recall@3为0.849，生成得分为2.746。

Conclusion: SAGE证明了解析细粒度实体并进行层次化多向量表示，比传统单向量区域编码更有效地解决密集文档图像中的细粒度检索问题，为实体级视觉检索提供了新思路。

Abstract: Dense document images often contain many fine-grained visual and textual entities whose relevance depends on a user query. Standard vision-language retrievers encode cropped regions with a single vector, which can mix distinct entity signals and obscure the evidence needed for fine-grained retrieval. We call this failure mode Semantic Dilution and quantitatively show that it degrades entity-level retrieval as a function of entity density. To mitigate it, we propose SAGE, a training-free framework that parses semantic entities from dense document images, represents them as hierarchical graph nodes with multi-vector embeddings, and retrieves query-relevant evidence through iterative entity-level subgraph matching. We also introduce DEAR, a dataset of 1,055 query--image pairs sourced from product detail pages, where each query requires retrieving and comparing multiple fine-grained entities from visually dense inputs across four question types of increasing complexity. Experiments show that SAGE substantially reduces semantic dilution and outperforms patch-level and OCR-based retrieval baselines on DEAR, achieving a Recall@3 of 0.849 and a generation score of 2.746 on multi-entity visual comparison queries. Our code is available at https://github.com/All4Nothing/SAGE.

</details>


### [374] [MURAL: Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04574)
*Ahmad Mousavi,Majid Alikhani,Yeon-Chang Lee,Roberto Corizzo,Yeganeh Abdollahinejad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MURAL是一个统一的多模态图神经网络推荐框架，通过自适应边学习和不确定性感知融合，将推荐从固定结构增强转向动态拓扑发现，在TikTok和Amazon等大规模基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态GNN推荐系统面临两大瓶颈：(1)结构性僵化——依赖静态预计算的相似度图，无法适应不断演化的用户偏好；(2)语义脆弱性——噪声模态信号被不加区分地融合，扭曲了协作信号。

Method: MURAL提出两核心模块：Adaptive Edge Learner结合可微检索增强策略与近似最近邻搜索(ANN)，以O(NlogN)复杂度发现潜在的商品-商品关联；Uncertainty-Aware Fusion模块建模异构模态的偶然不确定性(aleatoric uncertainty)，动态降低不可靠特征的权重；另采用对比式师生对齐将模态特定表示锚定到稳定行为信号上。

Result: 在TikTok和Amazon等大规模基准上，MURAL显著超越结构性和生成式最先进基线，实现更高精度，并在极端数据损坏下保持鲁棒性，同时通过模态主导性提供可解释性。

Conclusion: MURAL证明多模态推荐系统应从固定结构增强转向自适应动态拓扑发现，不确定性与对比对齐机制能有效提升推荐的准确性、可解释性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal Graph Neural Networks have become standard for recommendation by augmenting sparse interaction data with content features. Yet current architectures face two bottlenecks: structural rigidity, from a reliance on static precomputed similarity graphs that cannot adapt to evolving preferences; and semantic fragility, where noisy modality signals are indiscriminately fused, distorting the collaborative signal. We propose MURAL (Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning), a unified framework that shifts multimodal recommendation from fixed structural augmentation to dynamic topology discovery. To address structural rigidity, an Adaptive Edge Learner combines a differentiable retrieval-augmented strategy with an approximate nearest neighbor search to discover latent item-item correlations that are both semantically adaptive and computationally scalable (O(NlogN)). To address semantic fragility, an Uncertainty-Aware Fusion module models the aleatoric uncertainty of heterogeneous modalities, dynamically down-weighting unreliable features while prioritizing high-confidence signals as a defense against cross-modal noise. We further employ a contrastive teacher-student alignment that anchors modality-specific representations to stable behavioral signals, ensuring optimization stability without gradient leakage. Experiments on large-scale benchmarks including TikTok and Amazon show that MURAL significantly surpasses both structural and generative state-of-the-art baselines, achieving superior accuracy while offering interpretability through domain-specific modality dominance and robustness under extreme data corruption.

</details>


### [375] [Latent-Aligned Reasoning for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04645)
*Jiarui Jin,Anyang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LARK框架，通过两阶段潜在对齐推理机制解决多模态大模型在推荐任务中跨模态信号随推理步骤稀释的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态VLMs在推荐应用中存在根本性挑战：当表示通过多步推理传播时，视觉和文本信号会逐渐衰减，作者将其命名为"跨模态稀释"（cross-modal dilution）现象。

Method: LARK采用两阶段潜在推理框架：(1) 第一阶段将可学习潜在token与多步思维链推理交错，并与冻结的视觉编码器显式对齐，作为视觉检查点保留感知细节；(2) 第二阶段通过桥接MLP投影潜在表示并训练item对item对比学习，同时将中间特征与第一阶段的思维链隐藏状态对齐，防止推理语义衰减。

Result: 在三个公开基准和一个工业数据集上，LARK在多种推荐架构中达到最先进性能，控制消融实验确认了各组件各自的贡献。

Conclusion: LARK通过显式的潜在对齐机制有效缓解了跨模态稀释问题，验证了推理过程保留视觉与文本信号对提升推荐性能的关键作用。

Abstract: Multimodal Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in cross-modal understanding, yet a fundamental challenge persists when applying them to recommendation: as representations propagate through multi-step reasoning, both visual and textual signals progressively attenuate - a phenomenon we term cross-modal dilution. To address this, we propose LARK (Latent-Aligned Reasoning frameworK), a two-stage latent reasoning framework with complementary alignment mechanisms within a single VLM. In the first stage, learnable latent tokens are interleaved with multi-step chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and explicitly aligned with a frozen vision encoder, serving as visual checkpoints that preserve perceptual details throughout the reasoning chain. In the second stage, the latent representations are projected via a bridge MLP and trained with item-to-item contrastive learning; to prevent the reasoning semantics from fading, intermediate features are aligned with the CoT hidden states from the first stage, anchoring the final embeddings to the model's own reasoning output. Experiments on three public benchmarks and one industrial dataset show that LARK achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple recommendation architectures, with controlled ablations confirming the distinct contribution of each component.

</details>


### [376] [VizIt: A multi-view framework for exploring single-cell, spatial, and genetic data online](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04658)
*Chenhang Christopher Zhang,Yanqing Lou,Jie Yuan,Mingming Lu,Jacob Parker,Himanshu Chintalapudi,Zechuan Lin,Clemens R. Scherzer,Yuxuan Hu,Ruifeng Hu,Xianjun Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VizIt是一个开源框架，用于多视图交互式探索单细胞和空间转录组、表观基因组及遗传数据，将基因、细胞类型、条件、空间、基因组区域和变异中心视图连接起来，实现跨生物学视角的无缝导航，并通过帕金森细胞图谱进行了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学研究日益需要从互补的生物学视角审视数据，但当前交互式探索工具在不同模态和工具之间仍然碎片化，缺乏统一的探索框架。

Method: 开发VizIt开源框架，将六种中心视角（基因、细胞类型、条件、空间、基因组区域、变异）相互连接，支持单细胞和空间转录组、表观基因组和遗传数据的跨视图导航，并构建了帕金森细胞图谱作为可定制的多组学交互资源。

Result: 通过帕金森细胞图谱展示了VizIt在多组学数据多视角探索中的可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: VizIt为多组学数据提供统一的交互式多视图探索框架，弥合了不同生物学模态和视角之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Multi-omic studies increasingly require data to be examined from complementary biological perspectives, yet interactive exploration remains fragmented across modalities and tools. We present VizIt, an open-source framework for multi-view exploration of single-cell and spatial transcriptomic, epigenomic and genetic data. VizIt connects gene-, cell type-, condition-, spatial-, genomic region- and variant-centered views, enabling seamless navigation across biological perspectives. We demonstrate VizIt through the Parkinson's Cell Atlas, a customizable interactive multi-omic resource.

</details>


### [377] [Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization for Training-Free AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04813)
*Wei Zhou,Tiandeng Wu,Jiandong Ding,Zhufeng Fan,Yi Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出IGPO（Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization），一种基于库存锚定的策略级优化方法，用于固定AI搜索流水线。该方法将策略与环境事实分离，通过在线探测库存构建库存画像、离线基于查询分组随机回滚获取对比信号并利用库存引导的探索循环区分漏检路由与无匹配支持的情况。在商业智能助手AI搜索系统中部署，14天在线A/B测试显示CTR提升3.17%，审计不良案例减少38.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI搜索系统早期部署时面对频繁更新的商品目录，可用物品及其属性不能视为稳定知识，无法编码在固定提示或策略中。微调、强化学习和静态提示修补均不合适：标签稀缺、奖励随库存漂移、模型发布成本高、提示修复易过时。

Method: IGPO将策略与环境事实分离。在线阶段：通过学习生成的Policy Guidelines（策略准则）指导基于运行时库存证据的行动而非记忆物品，探测库存并构建库存画像，将相关准则注入检索和选择提示。离线阶段：随机回滚按查询分组，混合结果组直接产生对比信号；库存引导的探索循环区分漏检路由与在观察库存证据下无匹配支持的情况。

Result: 自2026年5月起部署于商业智能助手AI搜索系统。14天在线A/B测试显示：CTR相对提升3.17%，审计不良案例减少38.9%。

Conclusion: IGPO为动态库存环境下的AI搜索提供了一种免训练的优化方案，通过将策略学习与动态环境事实解耦，有效解决了标签稀缺、奖励漂移和提示过时等传统方法的固有缺陷，并在真实商业系统中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Early in deployment, an AI search system typically operates over a frequently updated product catalog, so the available items and their properties cannot be treated as stable knowledge that can be encoded in fixed prompts or strategies. Fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and static prompt patches fit poorly: labels are scarce, rewards drift with inventory, model releases are costly, and prompt fixes quickly stale. We present Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization (IGPO), a training-free approach for fixed AI search pipelines. IGPO separates policy from environment facts: it learns Policy Guidelines for acting on runtime inventory evidence rather than memorizing available items. Online, IGPO grounds each query by probing the inventory and constructing an inventory portrait, then injects relevant Policy Guidelines into the retrieval and selection prompts. Offline, stochastic rollouts are grouped by query -- mixed outcome groups directly yield contrastive signal, and an inventory-guided exploration loop distinguishes missed retrieval routes from cases where no matching support is found under the observed inventory evidence. Since May 2026, IGPO has been deployed in a commercial smart-assistant AI search system. A 14-day online A/B test of the complete IGPO treatment shows a 3.17% relative CTR lift and a 38.9% reduction in audited bad cases.

</details>


### [378] [Personalized Task Dependency Graphs for Mitigating Signal Erosion in Multi-Task Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04862)
*Fuyuan Liu,Tiandeng Wu,Yaqun Fang,Wei Zhou,Zehao Zhou,Wenping Chen,Qishun Mei,Jiaxin Zhou,Heng Chang,Yi Cao,Jiandong Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化任务依赖图（PTDG）方法，通过动态重连多任务之间的依赖强度来缓解信号侵蚀问题，结合GCN传播和自适应渐进掩码策略，在稀疏深层漏斗目标上显著提升性能（AUC最高提升1.45%），并在线A/B测试中实现CVR提升1.2%、eCPM提升1.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统中多目标优化面临信号侵蚀问题。现有MTL方法在静态转换漏斗上对所有任务施加统一强度的依赖，忽略了任务相关性会随物品特征自然变化；同时沿固定链路的层次化消息传递会导致累积信号衰减，损害稀疏深层漏斗目标的性能。

Method: PTDG方法：1）在尊重必要物理因果约束（如Click->Pay）的前提下，通过低秩近似为每个物品动态"重连"任务依赖路径的强度，确保结构鲁棒性；2）采用带硬因果掩码的GCN传播来构建自适应的信息捷径；3）引入自适应渐进掩码（APM）策略，根据任务稀疏度解耦共享参数，以稳定优化过程。

Result: 在KuaiRand1K和工业数据集上，PTDG在稀疏转换任务上AUC最高提升1.45%，在密集目标上保持相当性能。在线A/B测试显示，相比基线，CVR提升1.2%，eCPM提升1.9%。

Conclusion: 通过动态个性化任务依赖关系和自适应参数解耦，PTDG有效缓解了多任务学习中固定依赖结构带来的信号衰减问题，在保持密集目标性能的同时显著改善了稀疏深层漏斗目标的优化效果。

Abstract: Optimizing multiple conversion objectives is a core challenge in industrial recommendation, often limited by signal erosion in rigid architectures. Existing Multi-Task Learning (MTL) methods typically enforce uniform dependency strengths across a static conversion funnel, overlooking how task correlations naturally vary based on item characteristics. Hierarchical message passing along these fixed chains leads to cumulative signal attenuation, which degrades performance on sparse, deep-funnel objectives. To address this, we propose the Personalized Task Dependency Graphs (PTDG). While respecting necessary physical causal constraints (e.g., Click -> Pay), PTDG dynamically "rewires" the intensity of dependency pathways for each item via low-rank approximation to ensure structural robustness. We implement a GCN-based propagation with hard causal masking to establish adaptive information shortcuts. Additionally, we introduce an Adaptive Progressive Masking (APM) strategy that decouples shared parameters according to task sparsity, helping to stabilize optimization. Experiments on KuaiRand1K and an industrial dataset show that PTDG significantly improves AUC on sparse conversion tasks by up to 1.45%, while maintaining comparable performance on dense objectives. Online A/B testing shows PTDG improves Conversion Rate (CVR) by 1.2% and effective Cost Per Mille (eCPM) by 1.9% relative to the baseline.

</details>


### [379] [AtomRec: Evolving Atomic Memory for Agentic Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04882)
*Peiyu Hu,Weihai Lu,Siying Gu,Zhuodong Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Yuean Niu,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AtomRec，一个具有演化原子协作记忆的智能推荐系统。它将用户和物品记忆表示为结构化原子单元，通过语义链接和动态演化保留细粒度偏好阶段，在推荐时检索多跳证据路径而非孤立摘要，在四个公开基准上平均提升约8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能推荐系统的记忆机制存在两大缺陷：一是将用户和物品信息压缩为粗糙摘要，难以保留细粒度的偏好阶段；二是用标量协作链接连接记忆，当用户兴趣演化时难以检索可解释的证据。

Method: AtomRec将用户和物品记忆表示为结构化原子单元（atomic units），在不同相关记忆之间构建语义链接；当新交互到来时，演化更新相关历史字段。推荐阶段通过检索链接记忆形成多跳证据路径，而非孤立的邻居摘要，使协作信号支撑有依据的排序。

Result: 在四个公开基准上进行实验，AtomRec持续优于最先进的智能体和记忆增强基线，各项指标平均相对提升约8.5%。

Conclusion: AtomRec通过原子化记忆表示与多跳证据路径检索，有效解决了智能推荐系统中记忆粒度不足和证据可解释性差的问题，验证了演化式原子协作记忆在推荐任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems use large language models to maintain semantic memory and support evidence-aware recommendation. However, existing memory mechanisms often compress user and item information into coarse summaries and connect them with scalar collaborative links, making it difficult to preserve fine-grained preference stages or retrieve interpretable evidence as user interests evolve. We propose \textsc{AtomRec}, an agentic recommender with evolving atomic collaborative memory. \textsc{AtomRec} represents user and item memories as structured atomic units, builds semantic links across related memories, and evolves related historical fields when new interactions arrive. During recommendation, it retrieves linked memories as multi-hop evidence paths rather than isolated neighbor summaries, allowing collaborative signals to support grounded ranking. Experiments on four public benchmarks show that \textsc{AtomRec} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art agentic and memory-augmented baselines, with around 8.5\% average relative improvement across metrics.

</details>


### [380] [SAM-D2Q: Aligning Multimodal Doc2Query with Search Demand and Conversion for E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04961)
*Hui Zhou,Jian Hui Ji,Lei Ma,Rong Xiao,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了SAM-D2Q，一个面向电商搜索的业务对齐多模态Doc2Query框架，通过三阶段训练（任务自适应多模态SFT、多模态数据增强、基于RL的商业偏好对齐）来生成与用户意图和商业价值匹配的伪查询，在AliExpress上线后GMV提升3.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Doc2Query仅基于文本，无法利用产品图像中的视觉属性，且生成的扩展可能语义可行但商业效果不佳，未能针对电商搜索的业务目标进行优化。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1)任务自适应多模态监督微调增强模型对标题、图像和查询的理解；(2)多模态数据增强提升对关键视觉属性的感知及扩展覆盖；(3)基于强化学习的偏好对齐，引导模型生成更符合用户意图与商业价值的伪查询，适用于布尔检索约束。

Result: 离线实验显示检索性能显著优于传统Doc2Query方法；在AliExpress生产搜索系统中部署后，GMV提升+3.38%，Pay Count提升+2.27%。

Conclusion: 多模态信息与业务目标对齐对电商搜索文档扩展至关重要，SAM-D2Q验证了在真实生产环境中该方法的有效性和商业价值。

Abstract: E-commerce search often suffers from vocabulary mismatch between user queries and merchant-authored product titles, since short titles cannot fully cover diverse user expressions or visual product attributes. Although Doc2Query alleviates this issue by generating pseudo-queries for document expansion, traditional methods are text-only and not optimized for e-commerce business objectives. As a result, they may produce semantically plausible but commercially ineffective expansions and miss key attributes present in product images. To this end, we propose E-commerce Search-Aligned Multimodal Doc2Query (SAM-D2Q), a business-aligned multimodal document expansion framework for e-commerce search under Boolean retrieval constraints. SAM-D2Q consists of three stages: (1) task-adapted multimodal supervised fine-tuning to enhance vision-language understanding of product titles, images, and user queries; (2) multimodal data augmentation to improve perception of key visual attributes and expansion coverage; and (3) reinforcement-learning-based preference alignment toward search business objectives, encouraging the model to generate pseudo-queries that better match user intent and commercial value. Offline experiments show that SAM-D2Q substantially improves retrieval performance over traditional Doc2Query methods. Deployed in the AliExpress production search system, SAM-D2Q improves online business metrics, increasing GMV by +3.38% and Pay Count by +2.27%.

</details>


### [381] [Repeated Queries Exhaust an LLM's Brand Recommendations but Not Its Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05059)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本论文研究语言模型在重复相同购买问题时，其品牌推荐是否会被耗尽，取决于检索的使用。通过300个"问题-引擎"单元（50个问题×6个引擎×15次运行，对1470个裁决组织进行开放提取），发现不带网络搜索的5个引擎在第15次运行时仍有86-92%的单元持续出现未见过的品牌；而启用检索的引擎则收敛列表（中位数8个组织，64%的单元仍在增加）。引用域积累在所有测试范围持续上升，单一运行展示五轮品牌集的62-77%。研究使用精确稀疏和Chao2丰富度估计，并证明固定名册提取会人为制造平台期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在重复购买问题中是否会穷尽品牌推荐，以及检索（web search）如何影响这一过程。现有基于固定名册的追踪方法可能制造虚假的平台期，需要更开放的提取方法来揭示真实行为。

Method: 构建300个"问题-引擎"单元（50个问题×6个引擎×15次运行），其中5个引擎无网络搜索、1个引擎启用检索。对1470个裁决组织进行开放式提取，使用精确稀疏（exact rarefaction）和Chao2丰富度估计，并通过平行固定名册提取作为对照验证。

Result: 不带检索的引擎在15次运行后仍持续添加从未见过的品牌（86-92%单元），中位数品牌字汇表（repertoire）为15-31个组织；检索引擎收敛（中位数8个组织）。引用域在24次运行后仍持续增长，仅观察到Chao2下界估计的59-84%。单次运行仅展示五轮品牌集的62-77%，不同引擎间品牌重叠低（中位数38个组织中有15个仅在一个引擎出现）。固定名册提取产生平坦曲线，开放提取则消除该平台期。

Conclusion: 语言模型的品牌推荐开放性取决于是否使用检索：无检索引擎的品牌输出不受轮次限制地持续扩展，检索引擎则更快收敛。固定名册追踪方法人为制造平台期假象，开放式提取更能反映模型真实行为。重复购买场景的品牌样本可能本质上是不饱和的。

Abstract: Whether repeated identical buying questions exhaust a language model's brand recommendations depends on retrieval. Across 300 question-engine cells (50 questions, six engines, 15 runs each, open extraction over 1,470 adjudicated organizations), the five engines answering without web search were still adding never-seen brands at run 15 in 86-92% of cells, with median repertoires of 15-31 organizations; the one retrieval-enabled engine closed its list (median 8 organizations, 64% of cells still adding), matching four earlier deep cells where web-search runs saturated by run ten. Cited-domain accumulation keeps rising at every horizon tested: four deep cells were still adding domains at run 24 with 59-84% of the Chao2 lower-bound estimate observed, and 44% of the retrieval engine's breadth cells were still adding domains at run 15. A single run shows 62-77% of the five-run brand set, and across engines the median question draws 38 organizations, of which a median of 15 appear in exactly one engine. Estimators are exact rarefaction and Chao2 richness; a parallel fixed-roster extraction reproduces flat curves on identical responses, so roster-bounded tracking manufactures plateaus that open extraction removes.

</details>


### [382] [Beyond Co-purchase Relation: Evolution of Complementary Recommendations at Allegro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05063)
*Aleksandra Osowska-Kurczab,Klaudia Nazarko,Eliška Kosturová,Lidia Wojciechowska,Michał Bień*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AlleCompanion 框架，部署于 Allegro.com，用于互补商品推荐。该框架结合数据级过滤启发式与类别约束的双塔架构，将嘈杂的共购行为信号转化为精确的语义兼容性，并引入 ComCat 多源互补类别映射模块，整合专家规则、人工反馈、LLM 推理与统计挖掘。系统月服务超 2000 万活跃用户，在归因 GMV 与赞助位收入上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准推荐模型难以区分"仅仅被共同购买"的商品与"真正能搭配使用"的商品，互补商品推荐（如相机配镜头而非配三脚架或另一机身）对购物篮建设至关重要，但大规模共购数据中存在固有噪声，需要有效的消噪与语义兼容建模方法。

Method: 1) 数据层面：采用过滤启发式降低共购流量噪声；2) 模型层面：提出类别约束的双塔架构（Two Tower），其中 Category Adapter 在嵌入空间中引导模型，将候选限制在逻辑互补的类别边界内；3) 引入 ComCat（多源互补类别映射），作为翻译层从噪声流量中提炼有意义模式，整合专家规则、人类参与反馈、基于 LLM 的推理和统计挖掘，实现可维护、可控的解决方案。

Result: 实验表明，将显式类别级约束与神经架构结合能有效过滤共购噪声，提供满足真实用户需求的推荐。系统服务于每月超 2000 万活跃用户，有机发现场景归因 GMV 显著提升，赞助位也实现可观收入增长。

Conclusion: 显式的类别级约束与神经网络架构结合，是解决大规模互补商品推荐中噪声问题的有效途径；AlleCompanion 在生产环境中验证了方法与系统的可行性和商业价值。

Abstract: When a customer adds a professional camera to their cart, should the system suggest a matching lens, a generic tripod, or another camera body? Complementary Product Recommendation is vital for comprehensive basket building, yet standard models often fail to distinguish between items that are merely bought together and those that truly work together. In this paper, we present AlleCompanion: a production-scale retrieval framework deployed at Allegro.com that transforms noisy behavioural signals into precise semantic compatibility. We mitigate the intrinsic noise in large-scale co-purchase traffic by combining data-level filtering heuristics with a category-constrained Two Tower architecture. Within this framework, the Category Adapter guides the model in the embedding space, constraining candidates within logically complementary boundaries. Since modelling authentic user behaviour at scale is inherently difficult, we introduce ComCat, a multi-source Complementary Categories Mapping. ComCat acts as a translational layer that distils meaningful patterns from noisy traffic into a maintainable and controllable solution, integrating expert rules, human-in-the-loop feedback, LLM-based reasoning, and statistical mining. Our experimental results demonstrate that combining explicit category-level constraints with neural architectures effectively filters out co-purchase noise to surface recommendations that satisfy real-world user needs. Serving over 20 million active users monthly, the framework delivers significant uplifts in attributed GMV for organic discovery and drives substantial revenue growth in sponsored placements.

</details>


### [383] [Embedding Surgery: Localized Updates for Adaptive Ranking Correction in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05110)
*Maddalena Amendola,Antonio Mallia,Raffaele Perego*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"embedding surgery"（嵌入手术），一种轻量级的稠密检索自适应排序修正方法。它在查询时对选定的文档嵌入进行局部最小化更新，以融入编辑反馈、用户交互或大语言模型伪标签，并将该过程建模为凸优化问题。在TREC和MS MARCO基准上取得显著提升（DL-Hard上nDCG@10相对提升高达+60.64%），计算成本低，且不破坏嵌入空间全局结构，可直接在ANN索引上进行原地覆盖更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索系统在离线阶段计算并存储文档嵌入到静态索引中，导致系统无法适应用户反馈或不断演变的搜索意图。这一根本局限使得检索系统缺乏灵活性，难以根据反馈动态调整排序结果。

Method: 提出embedding surgery方法：1）在查询时对选定的文档嵌入施加局部最小更新；2）将修正建模为带排序约束的凸优化问题，在最小化嵌入修改量的同时满足排序约束；3）可利用编辑反馈、用户交互或LLM伪标签作为指导信号；4）通过原地覆盖方式兼容近似最近邻(ANN)索引，避免昂贵的索引重建。

Result: 在TREC Deep Learning、TREC Robust、TREC CAsT和MS MARCO基准上取得一致改进，DL-Hard上编辑反馈下nDCG@10相对提升高达+60.64%；即使在噪声或漂移反馈下仍具鲁棒性；修正效果可传播到语义相关的查询；embedding surgery可与CoRocchio等查询适应方法互补，获得额外增益且对噪声反馈更鲁棒。

Conclusion: Embedding surgery是一种轻量、高效且可扩展的稠密检索自适应方法，能在不破坏嵌入空间结构、不需重建索引的前提下实现有效的排序修正，弥补了稠密检索系统静态索引的局限，并与现有查询端适应方法形成有效互补。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems are core components of modern search engines, recommendation platforms, and retrieval-augmented generation pipelines. They encode documents and queries into dense embeddings, enabling efficient semantic search via vector similarity. However, because document embeddings are computed offline and stored in static indexes, these systems struggle to adapt to user feedback or evolving search intent. To address this limitation, we introduce \emph{embedding surgery}, a lightweight approach for adaptive ranking correction in dense retrieval. The method applies localized, minimal updates to selected document embeddings at query time, guided by editorial feedback, user interactions, or pseudo-labels from large language models. We formulate embedding surgery as a convex optimization problem that enforces ranking constraints while minimizing modifications to the affected document representations. We integrate embedding surgery into standard dense retrieval pipelines and evaluate it on TREC Deep Learning, TREC Robust, TREC CAsT, and MS MARCO benchmarks. Results show consistent improvements (e.g., up to +60.64\% relative improvement in nDCG@10 on DL-Hard under editorial feedback), even under noisy or shifting feedback, with low computational cost and without disrupting the global structure of the embedding space. Extensive experiments show that ranking corrections propagate to semantically related queries and that embedding updates can be applied safely and efficiently to scalable Approximate Nearest Neighbor indexes via simple in-place overwriting, without requiring costly index reconstruction. Finally, embedding surgery complements query adaptation methods such as CoRocchio, yielding additional gains while being more robust to noisy feedback.

</details>
